# 🏰 Fort Knox Authentication System

**Enterprise-grade, multi-tenant authentication system built with NestJS, TypeORM, and Redis**

[![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-MIT-blue.svg)](LICENSE)
[![TypeScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/TypeScript-5.3-blue)](https://www.typescriptlang.org/)
[![NestJS](https://img.shields.io/badge/NestJS-10-red)](https://nestjs.com/)

---

## 🚀 Features

### ✅ Core Authentication
- [x] Email/Password authentication with Argon2id hashing
- [x] JWT + Refresh Token implementation
- [x] Multi-tenant architecture
- [x] Session management with device tracking
- [ ] Social login (Google, GitHub, Microsoft)
- [ ] SAML 2.0 enterprise SSO
- [ ] OAuth 2.0 / OIDC server

### 🔐 Security
- [x] Rate limiting (Sliding Window + Token Bucket)
- [x] Brute force protection
- [x] AES-256-GCM field-level encryption
- [ ] Account lockout with exponential backoff
- [ ] Security headers (Helmet.js)
- [ ] CSRF protection
- [ ] IP whitelisting/blacklisting

### 🔑 Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA)
- [ ] TOTP (Google Authenticator, Authy)
- [ ] SMS OTP via Twilio
- [ ] Email OTP / Magic Links
- [ ] WebAuthn / FIDO2 (hardware keys, biometrics)
- [ ] Backup codes

### 👥 User & Tenant Management
- [x] Multi-tenant isolation
- [x] Custom domain support
- [ ] Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)
- [ ] Hierarchical permissions
- [ ] User invitation system
- [ ] Bulk user provisioning
- [ ] SCIM 2.0 user directory sync

### 📊 Admin Dashboard
- [ ] User management UI
- [ ] Analytics & reporting
- [ ] Security events monitoring
- [ ] Tenant configuration
- [ ] Branding customization

### 🔧 Developer Tools
- [ ] JavaScript/TypeScript SDK
- [ ] Python SDK
- [ ] Go SDK
- [ ] API documentation (Swagger/OpenAPI)
- [ ] Webhook system
- [ ] Developer portal

---

## 📦 Project Structure

```
fort-knox/
├── apps/
│   ├── auth-service/          # NestJS authentication microservice
│   ├── admin-portal/          # Next.js admin dashboard
│   ├── api-gateway/           # API gateway service
│   └── webhook-service/       # Webhook delivery system
├── packages/
│   ├── security/              # Security utilities (encryption, hashing, rate limiting)
│   ├── shared/                # Shared types, constants, DTOs
│   ├── database/              # TypeORM entities, migrations
│   ├── sdk-js/                # JavaScript/TypeScript SDK
│   └── ui/                    # shadcn/ui components
├── tools/
│   ├── scripts/               # Deployment scripts
│   └── docker/                # Docker configurations
├── turbo.json                 # Turborepo configuration
├── docker-compose.yml         # Local development services
└── package.json
```

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

### Backend
- **Framework:** NestJS (Node.js + TypeScript)
- **Database:** PostgreSQL 16
- **ORM:** TypeORM
- **Cache:** Redis 7
- **Queue:** Bull (Redis-based)

### Frontend
- **Framework:** Next.js 14 (App Router)
- **UI:** shadcn/ui + Tailwind CSS
- **State:** React Query

### Security
- **Password Hashing:** Argon2id
- **Encryption:** AES-256-GCM
- **JWT:** jsonwebtoken
- **Rate Limiting:** Redis-based sliding window

### DevOps
- **Containerization:** Docker
- **Orchestration:** Kubernetes
- **CI/CD:** GitHub Actions
- **Monitoring:** Prometheus + Grafana
- **Logging:** ELK Stack

---

## 🚀 Quick Start

### Prerequisites
- Node.js >= 20.0.0
- Docker & Docker Compose
- PostgreSQL 16 (or use Docker)
- Redis 7 (or use Docker)

### 1. Clone Repository
```bash
git clone https://github.com/your-org/fort-knox.git
cd fort-knox
```

### 2. Install Dependencies
```bash
npm install
```

### 3. Start Infrastructure (Docker)
```bash
npm run docker:dev
```

This starts:
- PostgreSQL on `localhost:5432`
- Redis on `localhost:6379`
- pgAdmin on `localhost:5050`
- Redis Commander on `localhost:8081`
- Mailhog on `localhost:8025`

### 4. Environment Variables
Create `.env` files in each app:

**apps/auth-service/.env:**
```env
# Database
DATABASE_HOST=localhost
DATABASE_PORT=5432
DATABASE_USER=fortknox
DATABASE_PASSWORD=fortknox_dev_password
DATABASE_NAME=fortknox_auth

# Redis
REDIS_HOST=localhost
REDIS_PORT=6379
REDIS_PASSWORD=fortknox_redis_password

# JWT
JWT_SECRET=your-super-secret-jwt-key-change-this
JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=your-super-secret-refresh-key-change-this

# Encryption
ENCRYPTION_KEY=your-256-bit-encryption-master-key

# Application
PORT=3001
NODE_ENV=development
```

### 5. Run Database Migrations
```bash
cd apps/auth-service
npm run db:migrate
npm run db:seed
```

### 6. Start Development Servers
```bash
# Start all apps in watch mode
npm run dev

# Or start specific app
npm run dev --filter=auth-service
```

---

## 📚 API Documentation

Once the auth-service is running, access Swagger docs at:
```
http://localhost:3001/api/docs
```

### Key Endpoints

#### Authentication
- `POST /auth/register` - Register new user
- `POST /auth/login` - Login
- `POST /auth/refresh` - Refresh access token
- `POST /auth/logout` - Logout
- `POST /auth/forgot-password` - Request password reset
- `POST /auth/reset-password` - Reset password

#### User Management
- `GET /users/me` - Get current user
- `PATCH /users/me` - Update current user
- `POST /users/change-password` - Change password

#### MFA
- `POST /mfa/totp/setup` - Setup TOTP
- `POST /mfa/totp/verify` - Verify TOTP code
- `POST /mfa/backup-codes` - Generate backup codes

---

## 🔐 Security Best Practices

### Implemented
✅ **Password Security**
- Argon2id hashing (OWASP recommended)
- Password strength validation
- Password history (prevent reuse)

✅ **Session Security**
- Device fingerprinting
- IP tracking
- Concurrent session limits

✅ **Rate Limiting**
- Per-IP rate limiting
- Per-user rate limiting
- Distributed rate limiting (Redis)

✅ **Encryption**
- AES-256-GCM for sensitive data
- HMAC signatures for webhooks
- Encrypted fields in database

### Planned
🔄 **Additional Security Measures**
- WAF (Web Application Firewall)
- DDoS protection
- Penetration testing
- Security audits
- Compliance certifications (SOC 2, ISO 27001)

## Project setup

```bash
$ yarn install
```

## Compile and run the project

```bash
# development
$ yarn run start

# watch mode
$ yarn run start:dev

# production mode
$ yarn run start:prod
```

## Run tests

```bash
# unit tests
$ yarn run test

# e2e tests
$ yarn run test:e2e

# test coverage
$ yarn run test:cov
```

## Deployment

When you're ready to deploy your NestJS application to production, there are some key steps you can take to ensure it runs as efficiently as possible. Check out the [deployment documentation](https://docs.nestjs.com/deployment) for more information.

If you are looking for a cloud-based platform to deploy your NestJS application, check out [Mau](https://mau.nestjs.com), our official platform for deploying NestJS applications on AWS. Mau makes deployment straightforward and fast, requiring just a few simple steps:

```bash
$ yarn install -g @nestjs/mau
$ mau deploy
```

With Mau, you can deploy your application in just a few clicks, allowing you to focus on building features rather than managing infrastructure.

FILE: apps/auth-service/consolidate.js
SIZE: 9.59 KB | LINES: 379

#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

/**
 * Code Consolidator Script
 * Recursively scans directories and consolidates all code files into a single text file
 */

class CodeConsolidator {
  constructor() {
    // Common code file extensions
    this.codeExtensions = new Set([
      '.js',
      '.jsx',
      '.ts',
      '.tsx',
      '.py',
      '.java',
      '.c',
      '.cpp',
      '.cc',
      '.cxx',
      '.cs',
      '.php',
      '.rb',
      '.go',
      '.rs',
      '.swift',
      '.kt',
      '.scala',
      '.r',
      '.m',
      '.mm',
      '.html',
      '.htm',
      '.css',
      '.scss',
      '.sass',
      '.less',
      '.xml',
      '.json',
      '.yaml',
      '.yml',
      '.toml',
      '.sh',
      '.bash',
      '.zsh',
      '.fish',
      '.ps1',
      '.bat',
      '.sql',
      '.graphql',
      '.proto',
      '.thrift',
      '.dockerfile',
      '.makefile',
      '.cmake',
      '.vue',
      '.svelte',
      '.dart',
      '.lua',
      '.pl',
      '.pm',
    ]);

    // Files to exclude
    this.excludeFiles = new Set([
      'package-lock.json',
      'yarn.lock',
      '.DS_Store',
      'Thumbs.db',
      '.env',
      '.env.local',
      '.env.production',
      '.env.development',
    ]);

    // Directories to exclude
    this.excludeDirs = new Set([
      'node_modules',
      '.git',
      '.svn',
      '.hg',
      'dist',
      'build',
      'target',
      'bin',
      'obj',
      '__pycache__',
      '.pytest_cache',
      'venv',
      'env',
      '.venv',
      '.env',
      'vendor',
      '.idea',
      '.vscode',
      '.vs',
      'coverage',
      '.nyc_output',
      '.cache',
    ]);

    this.stats = {
      filesProcessed: 0,
      totalLines: 0,
      skippedFiles: 0,
      errors: 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Check if file should be processed based on extension
   */
  isCodeFile(filePath) {
    const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
    const basename = path.basename(filePath).toLowerCase();

    // Special files without extensions
    const specialFiles = [
      'dockerfile',
      'makefile',
      'jenkinsfile',
      'readme',
      'license',
      'changelog',
    ];
    if (specialFiles.some((name) => basename.includes(name))) {
      return true;
    }

    return this.codeExtensions.has(ext);
  }

  /**
   * Check if file/directory should be excluded
   */
  shouldExclude(itemPath, isDirectory = false) {
    const basename = path.basename(itemPath);

    if (isDirectory) {
      return this.excludeDirs.has(basename) || basename.startsWith('.');
    }

    return (
      this.excludeFiles.has(basename) ||
      (basename.startsWith('.') && !this.isCodeFile(itemPath))
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get file size in a readable format
   */
  getFileSize(filePath) {
    try {
      const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
      const bytes = stats.size;

      if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
      const k = 1024;
      const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
      const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
      return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
    } catch (error) {
      return 'Unknown';
    }
  }

  /**
   * Process a single file and add its content to the output
   */
  processFile(filePath, outputStream) {
    try {
      const relativePath = path.relative(process.cwd(), filePath);
      const fileSize = this.getFileSize(filePath);
      const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');
      const lineCount = content.split('\n').length;

      // Write file header
      outputStream.write('\n' + '='.repeat(80) + '\n');
      outputStream.write(`FILE: ${relativePath}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`SIZE: ${fileSize} | LINES: ${lineCount}\n`);
      outputStream.write('='.repeat(80) + '\n\n');

      // Write file content
      outputStream.write(content);
      outputStream.write('\n\n');

      this.stats.filesProcessed++;
      this.stats.totalLines += lineCount;

      console.log(`✓ Processed: ${relativePath} (${lineCount} lines)`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`✗ Error processing ${filePath}: ${error.message}`);
      this.stats.errors++;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Recursively scan directory and process all code files
   */
  scanDirectory(dirPath, outputStream) {
    try {
      const items = fs.readdirSync(dirPath);

      for (const item of items) {
        const itemPath = path.join(dirPath, item);
        const stats = fs.statSync(itemPath);

        if (stats.isDirectory()) {
          if (!this.shouldExclude(itemPath, true)) {
            this.scanDirectory(itemPath, outputStream);
          } else {
            console.log(
              `⏭ Skipping directory: ${path.relative(process.cwd(), itemPath)}`,
            );
          }
        } else if (stats.isFile()) {
          if (
            this.isCodeFile(itemPath) &&
            !this.shouldExclude(itemPath, false)
          ) {
            this.processFile(itemPath, outputStream);
          } else {
            this.stats.skippedFiles++;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`✗ Error scanning directory ${dirPath}: ${error.message}`);
      this.stats.errors++;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Main consolidation function
   */
  consolidate(inputPath = '.', outputPath = 'consolidated-code.txt') {
    console.log('🚀 Starting code consolidation...\n');
    console.log(`Input: ${path.resolve(inputPath)}`);
    console.log(`Output: ${path.resolve(outputPath)}\n`);

    const outputStream = fs.createWriteStream(outputPath);

    // Write header
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    outputStream.write(`CODE CONSOLIDATION REPORT\n`);
    outputStream.write(`Generated: ${timestamp}\n`);
    outputStream.write(`Input Directory: ${path.resolve(inputPath)}\n`);
    outputStream.write(`${'='.repeat(80)}\n\n`);

    try {
      const inputStats = fs.statSync(inputPath);

      if (inputStats.isDirectory()) {
        this.scanDirectory(inputPath, outputStream);
      } else if (inputStats.isFile()) {
        this.processFile(inputPath, outputStream);
      } else {
        throw new Error('Input path is neither a file nor a directory');
      }

      // Write footer with statistics
      outputStream.write('\n' + '='.repeat(80) + '\n');
      outputStream.write('CONSOLIDATION SUMMARY\n');
      outputStream.write('='.repeat(80) + '\n');
      outputStream.write(`Files Processed: ${this.stats.filesProcessed}\n`);
      outputStream.write(
        `Total Lines: ${this.stats.totalLines.toLocaleString()}\n`,
      );
      outputStream.write(`Files Skipped: ${this.stats.skippedFiles}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`Errors: ${this.stats.errors}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`Generated: ${timestamp}\n`);

      outputStream.end();

      console.log('\n✅ Consolidation completed successfully!');
      console.log(`📊 Statistics:`);
      console.log(`   • Files processed: ${this.stats.filesProcessed}`);
      console.log(
        `   • Total lines: ${this.stats.totalLines.toLocaleString()}`,
      );
      console.log(`   • Files skipped: ${this.stats.skippedFiles}`);
      console.log(`   • Errors: ${this.stats.errors}`);
      console.log(`📁 Output saved to: ${path.resolve(outputPath)}`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`💥 Fatal error: ${error.message}`);
      outputStream.destroy();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Add custom file extensions
   */
  addExtensions(extensions) {
    extensions.forEach((ext) => {
      if (!ext.startsWith('.')) ext = '.' + ext;
      this.codeExtensions.add(ext.toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  /**
   * Add custom exclude patterns
   */
  addExcludes(files = [], dirs = []) {
    files.forEach((file) => this.excludeFiles.add(file));
    dirs.forEach((dir) => this.excludeDirs.add(dir));
  }
}

// CLI Usage
if (require.main === module) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const consolidator = new CodeConsolidator();

  // Parse command line arguments
  let inputPath = '.';
  let outputPath = 'consolidated-code.txt';
  let customExtensions = [];
  let excludeFiles = [];
  let excludeDirs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    switch (args[i]) {
      case '--input':
      case '-i':
        inputPath = args[++i];
        break;
      case '--output':
      case '-o':
        outputPath = args[++i];
        break;
      case '--extensions':
      case '-e':
        customExtensions = args[++i].split(',');
        break;
      case '--exclude-files':
        excludeFiles = args[++i].split(',');
        break;
      case '--exclude-dirs':
        excludeDirs = args[++i].split(',');
        break;
      case '--help':
      case '-h':
        console.log(`
Code Consolidator - Merge all code files into one text file

Usage: node consolidate.js [options]

Options:
  -i, --input <path>          Input directory or file (default: current directory)
  -o, --output <path>         Output file path (default: consolidated-code.txt)
  -e, --extensions <list>     Additional file extensions (comma-separated)
  --exclude-files <list>      Additional files to exclude (comma-separated)
  --exclude-dirs <list>       Additional directories to exclude (comma-separated)
  -h, --help                  Show this help

Examples:
  node consolidate.js
  node consolidate.js -i ./src -o output.txt
  node consolidate.js -e "md,txt" --exclude-dirs "temp,cache"
                `);
        process.exit(0);
    }
  }

  // Apply custom settings
  if (customExtensions.length) consolidator.addExtensions(customExtensions);
  if (excludeFiles.length || excludeDirs.length)
    consolidator.addExcludes(excludeFiles, excludeDirs);

  // Run consolidation
  consolidator.consolidate(inputPath, outputPath);
}

module.exports = CodeConsolidator;

SIZE: 1.44 KB | LINES: 59

export function PaginationMetadata(): MethodDecorator {
  return function (
    target: any,
    propertyKey: string | symbol,
    descriptor: PropertyDescriptor,
  ) {
    const originalMethod = descriptor.value;

    descriptor.value = async function (...args: any[]) {
      const { page = '1', size: pageSize = '10' } = args[0];

      const result = await originalMethod.apply(this, args);

      const startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
      const endIndex = startIndex + pageSize;

      const paginatedResults = result.data.slice(startIndex, endIndex);

      const metadata = {
        page,
        pageSize,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(result.data.length / pageSize),
        totalRecords: result.data.length,
      };

      return { metadata, results: paginatedResults };
    };

    return descriptor;
  };
}

export function PaginationMetadataUsers(): MethodDecorator {
  return function (
    target: any,
    propertyKey: string | symbol,
    descriptor: PropertyDescriptor,
  ) {
    const originalMethod = descriptor.value;

    descriptor.value = async function (...args: any[]) {
      const { page = '1', size: pageSize = '10' } = args[0];

      const result = await originalMethod.apply(this, args);

      const metadata = {
        page,
        pageSize,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(result.data.length / pageSize),
        totalRecords: result.data.length,
      };

      return { metadata, result };
    };

    return descriptor;
  };
}

import { IsOptional, IsInt, Min } from 'class-validator';
import { Type } from 'class-transformer';
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';

export class PaginationQueryDto {
  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  @Type(() => Number)
  @Min(1)
  @ApiProperty({ default: 1, description: 'Page number' })
  page?: number = 1;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  @Type(() => Number)
  @Min(1)
  @ApiProperty({ default: 10, description: 'Number of items per page' })
  limit?: number = 10;

  @IsOptional()
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Search query' })
  q?: string;
}

FILE: apps/auth-service/src/common/filters/http-exception.filter.ts
SIZE: 1.13 KB | LINES: 50

import {
  ExceptionFilter,
  Catch,
  ArgumentsHost,
  HttpException,
  HttpStatus,
} from '@nestjs/common';

@Catch(HttpException)
export class HttpExceptionFilter implements ExceptionFilter {
  catch(exception: HttpException, host: ArgumentsHost) {
    const ctx = host.switchToHttp();
    const response = ctx.getResponse();
    const status = exception.getStatus();
    const res = exception.getResponse() as
      | string
      | { message?: string | string[]; [key: string]: any };

    const message =
      typeof res === 'string'
        ? res
        : Array.isArray(res.message)
        ? res.message.join(', ')
        : res.message || 'Unexpected error';

    let code = 'INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR';
    switch (status) {
      case HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST:
        code = 'BAD_REQUEST';
        break;
      case HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED:
        code = 'UNAUTHORIZED';
        break;
      case HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN:
        code = 'FORBIDDEN';
        break;
      case HttpStatus.CONFLICT:
        code = 'CONFLICT';
        break;
    }

    response.status(status).json({
      error: {
        code,
        message,
      },
    });
  }
}

import {
  Injectable,
  ExecutionContext,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { AuthGuard } from "@nestjs/passport";
import { Observable } from "rxjs";

/**
 * JWT Authentication Guard
 *
 * Protects routes by validating JWT tokens
 */
@Injectable()
export class JwtAuthGuard extends AuthGuard("jwt") {
  canActivate(
    context: ExecutionContext,
  ): boolean | Promise<boolean> | Observable<boolean> {
    return super.canActivate(context);
  }

  handleRequest(err: any, user: any, info: any) {
    if (err || !user) {
      throw err || new UnauthorizedException("Invalid or expired token");
    }
    return user;
  }
}

import { Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from "@nestjs/common";
import { PassportStrategy } from "@nestjs/passport";
import { ExtractJwt, Strategy } from "passport-jwt";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";

/**
 * JWT Strategy
 *
 * Validates JWT tokens and extracts user payload
 */
@Injectable()
export class JwtStrategy extends PassportStrategy(Strategy) {
  constructor(private configService: ConfigService) {
    // @ts-ignore
    super({
      jwtFromRequest: ExtractJwt.fromAuthHeaderAsBearerToken(),
      ignoreExpiration: false,
      secretOrKey: configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
    });
  }

  async validate(payload: any) {
    if (!payload.sub) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid token payload");
    }

    return {
      sub: payload.sub,
      email: payload.email,
      tenantId: payload.tenantId,
    };
  }
}

FILE: apps/auth-service/src/common/guards/token-verification.guard.ts
SIZE: 1.93 KB | LINES: 72

import { CanActivate, ExecutionContext, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';
import { logger } from '../logger';

@Injectable()
export class TokenVerificationGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(private readonly jwtService: JwtService) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const token =
      request.cookies.accessToken ??
      request.headers['x-authorization'] ??
      request.headers['authorization'];

    if (!token) {
      return false;
    }

    try {
      const decoded = this.jwtService.verify(token, {
        secret: process.env.JWT_SECRET,
      });
      if (!decoded) {
        return false;
      }
      request.user = decoded;
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      return false;
    }
  }
}

@Injectable()
/**
 * Add Request User to the request object if present.
 * This guard is used to check if the user is authenticated.
 * If the user is authenticated, the user object is added to the request object.
 * If the user is not authenticated, the request object is not modified.
 * This will NOT block or PROTECT the route.
 *
 * If Protection is required, please use one of the above two Guards.
 */
export class RequestUserGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(private readonly jwtService: JwtService) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const token =
      request.cookies.accessToken ??
      request.headers['x-authorization'] ??
      request.headers['authorization'];

    try {
      if (token) {
        const decoded = this.jwtService.verify(token, {
          secret: process.env.JWT_SECRET,
        });
        if (decoded) request.user = decoded;
      }
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error({
        message: 'Failed to verify token',
        error,
      });
      return true;
    }
  }
}

import {
  NestInterceptor,
  ExecutionContext,
  CallHandler,
  Injectable,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';

@Injectable()
export class ResponseInterceptor implements NestInterceptor {
  intercept(context: ExecutionContext, next: CallHandler): Observable<any> {
    return next.handle().pipe(
      map((data) => {
        return {
          status: 200,
          data,
        };
      }),
    );
  }
}

SIZE: 3.7 KB | LINES: 145

// apps/auth-service/src/common/middleware/tenant-resolver.middleware.ts
import {
  Injectable,
  NestMiddleware,
  NotFoundException,
  Logger,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { TenantService } from "../../modules/tenant/tenant.service";
import { Tenant } from "@repo/database";

// Extend Express Request to include tenant
declare global {
  namespace Express {
    interface Request {
      tenant?: Tenant;
      tenantId?: string;
    }
  }
}

@Injectable()
export class TenantResolverMiddleware implements NestMiddleware {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(TenantResolverMiddleware.name);

  constructor(private tenantService: TenantService) {}

  async use(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
    try {
      const tenant = await this.resolveTenant(req);

      if (!tenant) {
        // Public endpoints (health, metrics) can proceed
        if (this.isPublicEndpoint(req.path)) {
          return next();
        }

        throw new NotFoundException(
          "Tenant not found. Please check your domain or tenant ID.",
        );
      }

      // Attach tenant to request
      req.tenant = tenant;
      req.tenantId = tenant.id;

      // Log for debugging (remove in production)
      this.logger.debug(
        `Resolved tenant: ${tenant.slug} (${tenant.id}) from ${req.hostname}`,
      );

      next();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Resolve tenant from request
   * Priority:
   * 1. Custom domain (auth.acme.com)
   * 2. Subdomain (acme.fortknox.io)
   * 3. X-Tenant-ID header (fallback)
   */
  private async resolveTenant(req: Request): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const hostname = req.hostname;

    // Strategy 1: Custom domain lookup
    // auth.acme.com → check if any tenant has this custom domain
    const customDomainTenant =
      await this.tenantService.findByCustomDomain(hostname);

    if (customDomainTenant) {
      return customDomainTenant;
    }

    // Strategy 2: Subdomain extraction
    // acme.fortknox.io → extract "acme"
    const subdomain = this.extractSubdomain(hostname);

    if (subdomain && subdomain !== "www" && subdomain !== "api") {
      const subdomainTenant = await this.tenantService.findBySlug(subdomain);

      if (subdomainTenant) {
        return subdomainTenant;
      }
    }

    // Strategy 3: X-Tenant-ID header (for development/testing)
    const tenantIdHeader = req.headers["x-tenant-id"] as string;

    if (tenantIdHeader) {
      // Try as ID first
      let tenant = await this.tenantService.findById(tenantIdHeader);

      // Try as slug if ID lookup fails
      if (!tenant) {
        tenant = await this.tenantService.findBySlug(tenantIdHeader);
      }

      if (tenant) {
        return tenant;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Extract subdomain from hostname
   * Examples:
   * - acme.fortknox.io → "acme"
   * - auth.acme.com → "auth"
   * - localhost:4000 → null
   */
  private extractSubdomain(hostname: string): string | null {
    // Remove port if present
    const host: string = hostname.split(":")[0] as string;

    // Localhost or IP address
    if (host === "localhost" || /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(host)) {
      return null;
    }

    const parts = host.split(".");

    // Need at least 3 parts for subdomain (sub.domain.tld)
    if (parts.length < 3) {
      return null;
    }

    // Return first part as subdomain
    return parts[0] as string;
  }

  /**
   * Check if endpoint is public (doesn't require tenant)
   */
  private isPublicEndpoint(path: string): boolean {
    const publicPaths = ["/health", "/metrics", "/.well-known", "/favicon.ico"];

    return publicPaths.some((publicPath) => path.startsWith(publicPath));
  }
}

import * as Joi from 'joi';

export const validationSchema = Joi.object({
  NODE_ENV: Joi.string()
    .valid('development', 'test', 'production')
    .default('development'),
  PORT: Joi.number().port().default(4000),

  ISSUER_BASE_URL: Joi.string()
    .uri({ scheme: ['http', 'https'] })
    .required(),
  TENANT_RESOLUTION: Joi.string().valid('path', 'subdomain').default('path'),

  JWT_ALG: Joi.string().valid('RS256', 'EdDSA').default('RS256'),
  JWKS_CACHE_SECONDS: Joi.number().integer().min(0).default(300),

  KEYRING_MASTER_KEY: Joi.string().base64().length(44).required(), // 32 bytes (base64 => 44 chars)

  DB_URL: Joi.string()
    .uri({ scheme: ['postgres', 'postgresql'] })
    .required(),
  DB_SSL: Joi.string()
    .valid('true', 'false', '1', '0', 'yes', 'no', 'on', 'off')
    .default('false'),
  DB_LOGGING: Joi.string()
    .valid('true', 'false', '1', '0', 'yes', 'no', 'on', 'off')
    .default('false'),

  REDIS_HOST: Joi.string().default('localhost'),
  REDIS_PORT: Joi.number().integer().min(1).default(6379),
  REDIS_USERNAME: Joi.string().allow(''),
  REDIS_PASSWORD: Joi.string().allow(''),
});

FILE: apps/auth-service/src/core/app.module.ts

import { Module, ValidationPipe } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { AppApiModule } from './modules/app-module';
import { DocumentBuilder, SwaggerModule } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { logger } from 'src/common/logger';
import { ResponseInterceptor } from 'src/common/interceptors/response.interceptors';
import { saveApiDoc } from './services/utility/utility.service';
import { ALLOWED_URLS } from '../common/constants';
import cookieParser from 'cookie-parser';
import session from 'express-session';

@Module({
  imports: [],
})
export class AppModule {
  static async startApp(): Promise<void> {
    const app = await NestFactory.create(AppApiModule, { bufferLogs: true });
    app.enableCors({
      origin: ALLOWED_URLS,
      methods: 'GET,HEAD,PUT,PATCH,POST,DELETE',
      optionsSuccessStatus: 200,
      credentials: true,
    });
    app.useGlobalPipes(
      new ValidationPipe({
        whitelist: true,
        transform: true,
      }),
    );

    const config = new DocumentBuilder()
      .setTitle('identity-server')
      .setDescription('API for Identity Server')
      .setVersion('1.0')
      .setExternalDoc('Sample', 'https://id.sample.com')
      .addBearerAuth()
      .build();

    const documentForGeneralApis = SwaggerModule.createDocument(app, config);

    SwaggerModule.setup('api-docs', app, documentForGeneralApis);
    saveApiDoc('api-docs', documentForGeneralApis, 'nest-be');

    if (app) {
      logger.info('Application started successfully');
      logger.info(`Application is running on: ${process.env.PORT}`);
      app.useGlobalInterceptors(new ResponseInterceptor());
      app.use(cookieParser());
      // app.setGlobalPrefix('api');
      app.use(
        session({
          secret: 'nest-session-secret',
          resave: false,
          saveUninitialized: false,
        }),
      );
      await app.listen(process.env.PORT || 4000);
    }
  }
}

import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { dataSourceOptions } from "@repo/database";

@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({
      imports: [ConfigModule],
      inject: [ConfigService],
      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) =>
        ({
          ...dataSourceOptions,
          host: configService.get("DATABASE_HOST"),
          port: configService.get("DATABASE_PORT"),
          username: configService.get("DATABASE_USER"),
          password: configService.get("DATABASE_PASSWORD"),
          database: configService.get("DATABASE_NAME"),
          logging: configService.get("DATABASE_LOGGING") === "true",
          autoLoadEntities: true,
          synchronize: false, // NEVER use in production
        }) as any,
    }),
  ],
})
export class DatabaseModule {}

import { Injectable } from "@nestjs/common";
import Redis from "ioredis";
import { CacheTTL } from "@/common/constants/cache";

type RedisConfig = {
  host: string;
  port: number;
  username?: string;
  password?: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class RedisCacheService {
  private readonly redis: Redis;

  constructor() {
    this.redis = new Redis(RedisCacheService.getConfig());
  }

  static getConfig() {
    const config: RedisConfig = {
      host: process.env.REDIS_HOST || "localhost",
      port: parseInt(process.env.REDIS_PORT || "6379", 10),
    };
    if (process.env.REDIS_USERNAME) {
      config.username = process.env.REDIS_USERNAME;
    }

    if (process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD) {
      config.password = process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD;
    }

    return config;
  }

  async get<T>(key: string): Promise<T | null> {
    const value = await this.redis.get(key);
    return value ? JSON.parse(value) : null;
  }

  async set(key: string, value: unknown, ttlSeconds = CacheTTL.threeDays) {
    await this.redis.set(key, JSON.stringify(value), "EX", ttlSeconds);
  }

  async update(key: string, value: unknown) {
    await this.redis.set(key, JSON.stringify(value), "KEEPTTL");
  }

  async del(key: string) {
    await this.redis.del(key);
  }

  async flush() {
    await this.redis.flushall();
  }

  async deleteByPattern(pattern: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const keys = await this.redis.keys(pattern);
    if (keys.length) await this.redis.del(...keys);
    return keys;
  }
}

SIZE: 986 B | LINES: 48

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import * as os from 'os';

@Injectable()
export class StatsService {
  private startTime: number;
  private requestCount: number;

  constructor() {
    this.startTime = Date.now();
    this.requestCount = 0;
  }

  getSystemStats() {
    const uptime = os.uptime();
    const loadAverage = os.loadavg();
    const memoryUsage = process.memoryUsage();
    const totalMemory = os.totalmem();
    const freeMemory = os.freemem();
    const cpuUsage = os.cpus();

    return {
      uptime,
      loadAverage,
      memoryUsage,
      totalMemory,
      freeMemory,
      cpuUsage,
    };
  }

  incrementRequestCount() {
    this.requestCount++;
  }

  getThroughput() {
    const currentTime = Date.now();
    const elapsedTime = (currentTime - this.startTime) / 1000; // in seconds
    const throughput = this.requestCount / elapsedTime;

    return {
      requestCount: this.requestCount,
      elapsedTime,
      throughput,
    };
  }
}

import { Injectable } from "@nestjs/common";
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "fs";

@Injectable()
export class UtilityService {}

export function saveApiDoc(
  fileName: string,
  document: any,
  folderName: string,
) {
  const dir = `./api-management/${folderName}`;
  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  }
  writeFileSync(`${dir}/${fileName}.yaml`, JSON.stringify(document, null, 2));
}

const yes = (v?: string, d = false) =>
  v === undefined
    ? d
    : ['1', 'true', 'yes', 'on'].includes(String(v).toLowerCase());

  type: 'postgres',
  url: process.env.DB_URL,
  ssl: yes(process.env.DB_SSL) ? { rejectUnauthorized: false } : false,

});

// Create seed script: apps/auth-service/src/database/seeds/tenants.seed.ts
import { DataSource } from "typeorm";
import { Tenant } from "@repo/database";

export async function seedTenants(dataSource: DataSource) {
  const tenantRepo = dataSource.getRepository(Tenant);

  // Create test tenants
  const tenants = [
    {
      slug: "acme",
      name: "Acme Corporation",
      environment: "production",
      active: true,
    },
    {
      slug: "startup",
      name: "Startup Inc",
      environment: "production",
      active: true,
    },
    {
      slug: "test",
      name: "Test Tenant",
      environment: "development",
      active: true,
    },
  ];

  for (const data of tenants) {
    const exists = await tenantRepo.findOne({ where: { slug: data.slug } });
    if (!exists) {
      await tenantRepo.save(tenantRepo.create(data));
      console.log(`✅ Created tenant: ${data.slug}`);
    }
  }
}

import {
  Controller,
  Post,
  Body,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  Ip,
  Headers,
  Get,
  UseGuards,
  Request,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiTags,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiBearerAuth,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { AuthService } from "./auth.service";
import {
  RegisterDto,
  LoginDto,
  ForgotPasswordDto,
  ResetPasswordDto,
} from "./auth.dto";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";

/**
 * Authentication Controller
 *
 * Handles all authentication-related endpoints:
 * - Registration
 * - Login
 * - Logout
 * - Password reset
 * - Email verification
 */
@ApiTags("Authentication")
@Controller("auth")
export class AuthController {
  constructor(private readonly authService: AuthService) {}

  /**
   * Register a new user
   */
  @Post("register")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Register a new user" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: "User successfully registered" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Bad request - validation failed" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: "User already exists" })
  async register(
    @Body() registerDto: RegisterDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    return this.authService.register({
      ...registerDto,
      ip,
      userAgent: userAgent || "unknown",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Login with email and password
   */
  @Post("login")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Login with email and password" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Login successful" })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Unauthorized - invalid credentials",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 423, description: "Account locked" })
  async login(
    @Body() loginDto: LoginDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    return this.authService.login({
      ...loginDto,
      ip,
      userAgent: userAgent || "unknown",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Logout - revoke session
   */
  @Post("logout")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Logout and revoke session" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Logout successful" })
  async logout(@Request() req: any) {
    await this.authService.logout(req.user.sub);
    return { message: "Logged out successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Request password reset
   */
  @Post("forgot-password")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Request password reset email" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Password reset email sent" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "User not found" })
  async forgotPassword(
    @Body() forgotPasswordDto: ForgotPasswordDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
  ) {
    await this.authService.forgotPassword(forgotPasswordDto.email, ip);
    return {
      message:
        "If an account exists with this email, a password reset link has been sent",
    };
  }

  /**
   * Reset password with token
   */
  @Post("reset-password")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Reset password using token" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Password reset successful" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Invalid or expired token" })
  async resetPassword(@Body() resetPasswordDto: ResetPasswordDto) {
    await this.authService.resetPassword(
      resetPasswordDto.token,
      resetPasswordDto.password,
    );
    return { message: "Password reset successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Verify email address
   */
  @Post("verify-email")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Verify email address" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Email verified successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Invalid or expired token" })
  async verifyEmail(@Body("token") token: string) {
    await this.authService.verifyEmail(token);
    return { message: "Email verified successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Resend verification email
   */
  @Post("resend-verification")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Resend email verification" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Verification email sent" })
  async resendVerification(@Body("email") email: string) {
    await this.authService.resendVerification(email);
    return { message: "Verification email sent" };
  }

  /**
   * Refresh access token
   */
  @Post("refresh")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Refresh access token" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Token refreshed successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: "Invalid refresh token" })
  async refresh(@Body("refreshToken") refreshToken: string) {
    return this.authService.refreshToken(refreshToken);
  }

  /**
   * Get current user info (for testing JWT)
   */
  @Get("me")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get current authenticated user" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "User info retrieved" })
  async getMe(@Request() req: any) {
    return this.authService.getCurrentUser(req.user.sub);
  }
}

import {
  IsEmail,
  IsString,
  MinLength,
  MaxLength,
  Matches,
  IsOptional,
  IsNotEmpty,
} from "class-validator";
import { ApiProperty } from "@nestjs/swagger";

/**
 * Register DTO
 */
export class RegisterDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "user@example.com" })
  @IsEmail()
  email!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "SecureP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(8)
  @MaxLength(128)
  @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[@$!%*?&])[A-Za-z\d@$!%*?&]/, {
    message:
      "Password must contain uppercase, lowercase, number and special character",
  })
  password!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "tenant-uuid", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  tenantId?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "John", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  firstName?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "Doe", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  lastName?: string;
}

/**
 * Login DTO
 */
export class LoginDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "user@example.com" })
  @IsEmail()
  email!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "SecureP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  password!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "tenant-uuid", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  tenantId?: string;
}

/**
 * Forgot Password DTO
 */
export class ForgotPasswordDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "user@example.com" })
  @IsEmail()
  email!: string;
}

/**
 * Reset Password DTO
 */
export class ResetPasswordDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "reset-token-here" })
  @IsString()
  token!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "NewSecureP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(8)
  @MaxLength(128)
  @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[@$!%*?&])[A-Za-z\d@$!%*?&]/, {
    message:
      "Password must contain uppercase, lowercase, number and special character",
  })
  password!: string;
}

/**
 * Verify Email DTO
 */
export class VerifyEmailDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "verification-token-here" })
  @IsString()
  token!: string;
}

FILE: apps/auth-service/src/modules/auth/auth.service.ts
SIZE: 18.92 KB | LINES: 702

import {
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
  BadRequestException,
  NotFoundException,
  ConflictException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, MoreThan } from "typeorm";
import { JwtService } from "@nestjs/jwt";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { User, Session, SecurityEvent, Tenant } from "@repo/database";
import { PasswordService } from "@repo/security";
import * as crypto from "crypto";

/**
 * Authentication Service (Updated for Multi-Tenant)
 *
 * Handles all authentication business logic with tenant scoping:
 * - User registration (tenant-scoped)
 * - Login/logout (tenant-scoped)
 * - Password reset (tenant-scoped)
 * - Email verification (tenant-scoped)
 * - Session management (tenant-scoped)
 * - Security event logging (tenant-scoped)
 */
@Injectable()
export class AuthService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private readonly userRepository: Repository<User>,
    @InjectRepository(Session)
    private readonly sessionRepository: Repository<Session>,
    @InjectRepository(SecurityEvent)
    private readonly securityEventRepository: Repository<SecurityEvent>,
    @InjectRepository(Tenant)
    private readonly tenantRepository: Repository<Tenant>,
    private readonly jwtService: JwtService,
    private readonly configService: ConfigService,
    private readonly passwordService: PasswordService,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Register a new user (TENANT REQUIRED)
   */
  async register(data: {
    tenantId: string; // ← Now REQUIRED!
    email: string;
    password: string;
    firstName?: string;
    lastName?: string;
    ip: string;
    userAgent: string;
  }) {
    // Validate tenant exists and is active
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.tenantId, active: true },
    });

    if (!tenant) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid or inactive tenant");
    }

    // Validate password strength
    const validation = PasswordService.validateStrength(data.password);

    if (!validation.isValid) {
      throw new BadRequestException({
        message: "Password does not meet security requirements",
        errors: validation.errors,
        score: validation.score,
      });
    }

    // Check if user already exists IN THIS TENANT
    const existingUser = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        email: data.email,
        tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Tenant-scoped lookup
      },
    });

    if (existingUser) {
      // Log failed registration attempt (tenant-scoped)
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
        eventType: "REGISTRATION_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: data.ip,
        userAgent: data.userAgent,
        metadata: { reason: "email_exists", email: data.email },
      });

      throw new ConflictException(
        "User with this email already exists in this tenant",
      );
    }

    // Hash password with Argon2id
    const passwordHash = await PasswordService.hash(data.password);

    // Generate email verification token
    const emailVerificationToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");

    // Create user with tenantId
    const user = this.userRepository.create({
      tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Always set tenant
      email: data.email,
      passwordHash,
      firstName: data.firstName,
      lastName: data.lastName,
      emailVerificationToken,
      emailVerificationExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), // 24 hours
    });

    const savedUser = await this.userRepository.save(user);

    // Log successful registration (tenant-scoped)
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: savedUser.id,
      tenantId: savedUser.tenantId,
      eventType: "USER_CREATED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: data.ip,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      metadata: { email: savedUser.email },
    });

    // TODO: Send verification email
    // await this.emailService.sendVerificationEmail(savedUser.email, emailVerificationToken);

    return {
      id: savedUser.id,
      email: savedUser.email,
      firstName: savedUser.firstName,
      lastName: savedUser.lastName,
      message:
        "Registration successful. Please check your email to verify your account.",
    };
  }

  /**
   * Login with email and password (TENANT REQUIRED)
   */
  async login(data: {
    tenantId: string; // ← Now REQUIRED!
    email: string;
    password: string;
    ip: string;
    userAgent: string;
  }) {
    // Find user IN THIS TENANT with password hash
    const user = await this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHash")
      .where("user.email = :email", { email: data.email })
      .andWhere("user.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId: data.tenantId }) // ← Tenant filter
      .getOne();

    if (!user) {
      // Log failed login (tenant-scoped)
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
        eventType: "LOGIN_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: data.ip,
        userAgent: data.userAgent,
        metadata: { reason: "user_not_found", email: data.email },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid email or password");
    }

    // Check if account is locked
    if (user.lockedUntil && user.lockedUntil > new Date()) {
      const minutesLeft = Math.ceil(
        (user.lockedUntil.getTime() - Date.now()) / 1000 / 60,
      );

      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId: user.id,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "LOGIN_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: data.ip,
        userAgent: data.userAgent,
        metadata: { reason: "account_locked", minutesLeft },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        `Account is locked. Please try again in ${minutesLeft} minute(s).`,
      );
    }

    // Verify password
    const isPasswordValid = await PasswordService.verify(
      user.passwordHash,
      data.password,
    );

    if (!isPasswordValid) {
      // Increment failed attempts
      await this.incrementFailedAttempts(data.tenantId, user.id);

      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId: user.id,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "LOGIN_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: data.ip,
        userAgent: data.userAgent,
        metadata: { reason: "invalid_password" },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid email or password");
    }

    // Reset failed attempts
    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId: data.tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        failedLoginAttempts: 0,
        lockedUntil: undefined,
        lastLoginAt: new Date(),
        lastLoginIp: data.ip,
      },
    );

    // Create session (tenant-scoped)
    const session = await this.createSession({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      userId: user.id,
      ipAddress: data.ip,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
    });

    // Generate JWT tokens (include tenantId in payload)
    const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(
      {
        sub: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        sessionId: session.id, // ← Include session ID
      },
      {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
        expiresIn: "15m", // 15 minutes
      },
    );

    const refreshToken = this.jwtService.sign(
      {
        sub: user.id,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        sessionId: session.id,
        type: "refresh",
      },
      {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_REFRESH_SECRET"),
        expiresIn: "7d", // 7 days
      },
    );

    // Log successful login (tenant-scoped)
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "LOGIN_SUCCESS",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: data.ip,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      metadata: { sessionId: session.id },
    });

    return {
      accessToken,
      refreshToken,
      user: {
        id: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        firstName: user.firstName,
        lastName: user.lastName,
        emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Logout - revoke current session (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async logout(tenantId: string, userId: string, sessionId?: string) {
    if (sessionId) {
      // Revoke specific session
      await this.sessionRepository.update(
        {
          id: sessionId,
          userId,
          tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
        },
        {
          revokedAt: new Date(),
          revokedReason: "user_logout",
        },
      );
    } else {
      // Revoke all active sessions for this user IN THIS TENANT
      await this.sessionRepository.update(
        {
          userId,
          tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
          revokedAt: null, // Only active sessions
        },
        {
          revokedAt: new Date(),
          revokedReason: "user_logout",
        },
      );
    }

    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      tenantId,
      eventType: "LOGOUT",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Request password reset (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async forgotPassword(tenantId: string, email: string, ip: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        email,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
    });

    // Don't reveal if user exists or not (security best practice)
    if (!user) {
      return;
    }

    // Generate reset token
    const resetToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const resetTokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(resetToken)
      .digest("hex");

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        passwordResetToken: resetTokenHash,
        passwordResetExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000), // 1 hour
      },
    );

    // Log password reset request
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: ip,
      userAgent: "system",
    });

    // TODO: Send password reset email
    // await this.emailService.sendPasswordResetEmail(user.email, resetToken);

    console.log("Password reset token:", resetToken); // For development
  }

  /**
   * Reset password with token (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async resetPassword(tenantId: string, token: string, newPassword: string) {
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");

    const user = await this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .addSelect("user.passwordResetToken")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHash")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHistory")
      .where("user.passwordResetToken = :tokenHash", { tokenHash })
      .andWhere("user.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId }) // ← Tenant filter
      .andWhere("user.passwordResetExpiresAt > :now", { now: new Date() })
      .getOne();

    if (!user) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid or expired password reset token");
    }

    // Validate new password
    const validation = PasswordService.validateStrength(newPassword);
    if (!validation.isValid) {
      throw new BadRequestException({
        message: "Password does not meet security requirements",
        errors: validation.errors,
      });
    }

    // Check password history
    const passwordHistory = user.passwordHistory || [];
    for (const oldHash of passwordHistory) {
      const isReused = await PasswordService.verify(oldHash, newPassword);
      if (isReused) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          "Password has been used recently. Please choose a different password.",
        );
      }
    }

    // Hash new password
    const newPasswordHash = await PasswordService.hash(newPassword);

    // Update password and history
    const updatedHistory = [user.passwordHash, ...passwordHistory].slice(0, 5);

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        passwordHash: newPasswordHash,
        passwordHistory: updatedHistory,
        passwordResetToken: undefined,
        passwordResetExpiresAt: undefined,
      },
    );

    // Log password reset
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });

    // Revoke all sessions IN THIS TENANT (force re-login)
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      {
        userId: user.id,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
        revokedAt: null,
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "password_reset",
      },
    );
  }

  /**
   * Verify email address (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async verifyEmail(tenantId: string, token: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
        emailVerificationToken: token,
        emailVerificationExpiresAt: MoreThan(new Date()),
      },
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid or expired verification token");
    }

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        emailVerified: true,
        emailVerificationToken: undefined,
        emailVerificationExpiresAt: undefined,
      },
    );

    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "EMAIL_VERIFIED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Resend verification email (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async resendVerification(tenantId: string, email: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        email,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
    });

    if (!user) {
      // Don't reveal if user exists
      return;
    }

    if (user.emailVerified) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Email is already verified");
    }

    // Generate new token
    const emailVerificationToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        emailVerificationToken,
        emailVerificationExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
      },
    );

    // TODO: Send verification email
    // await this.emailService.sendVerificationEmail(user.email, emailVerificationToken);

    console.log("Verification token:", emailVerificationToken); // For development
  }

  /**
   * Refresh access token (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async refreshToken(tenantId: string, refreshToken: string) {
    try {
      const payload = this.jwtService.verify(refreshToken, {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_REFRESH_SECRET"),
      });

      if (payload.type !== "refresh") {
        throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid refresh token");
      }

      // Verify tenant matches
      if (payload.tenantId !== tenantId) {
        throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid tenant for this token");
      }

      const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
        where: {
          id: payload.sub,
          tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
        },
      });

      if (!user) {
        throw new UnauthorizedException("User not found");
      }

      // Verify session is still active
      const session = await this.sessionRepository.findOne({
        where: {
          id: payload.sessionId,
          userId: user.id,
          tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
          revokedAt: null,
        },
      });

      if (!session) {
        throw new UnauthorizedException("Session has been revoked");
      }

      // Generate new access token
      const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(
        {
          sub: user.id,
          email: user.email,
          tenantId: user.tenantId,
          sessionId: session.id,
        },
        {
          secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
          expiresIn: "15m",
        },
      );

      return { accessToken };
    } catch (error) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid or expired refresh token");
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get current user (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async getCurrentUser(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: userId,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
      relations: ["tenant"],
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    return {
      id: user.id,
      email: user.email,
      firstName: user.firstName,
      lastName: user.lastName,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
      phoneVerified: user.phoneVerified,
      tenant: {
        id: user.tenant.id,
        name: user.tenant.name,
        slug: user.tenant.slug, // ← Use 'slug' not 'subdomain'
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Create session (TENANT SCOPED) - PRIVATE METHOD
   */
  private async createSession(data: {
    tenantId: string;
    userId: string;
    ipAddress?: string;
    userAgent?: string;
  }): Promise<Session> {
    const refreshTokenFamilyId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const deviceId = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex");

    const session = this.sessionRepository.create({
      tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Always set tenant
      userId: data.userId,
      refreshTokenFamilyId,
      deviceId,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), // 7 days
    });

    return this.sessionRepository.save(session);
  }

  /**
   * Increment failed login attempts (TENANT SCOPED) - PRIVATE METHOD
   */
  private async incrementFailedAttempts(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: userId,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
    });

    if (!user) return;

    const newAttempts = user.failedLoginAttempts + 1;

    // Lock account after 5 failed attempts
    const update: any = {
      failedLoginAttempts: newAttempts,
    };

    if (newAttempts >= 5) {
      update.lockedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000); // 15 minutes
    }

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: userId, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      update,
    );
  }

  /**
   * Log security event (TENANT SCOPED) - PRIVATE METHOD
   */
  private async logSecurityEvent(data: {
    userId?: string;
    tenantId: string; // ← Now REQUIRED!
    eventType: string;
    severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
    ipAddress: string;
    userAgent: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  }) {
    await this.securityEventRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Always set tenant
      userId: data.userId,
      eventType: data.eventType,
      severity: data.severity,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      metadata: data.metadata || {},
    });
  }
}

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/tenant/tenant.controller.ts
import {
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
  Patch,
  Body,
  Param,
  UseGuards,
  Req,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { type Request } from "express";
import { TenantService } from "./tenant.service";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";

// DTO for creating tenant
export class CreateTenantDto {
  slug!: string;
  name!: string;
  environment?: string;
}

export class SetCustomDomainDto {
  domain!: string;
}

@Controller("tenants")
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard) // Only authenticated users
export class TenantController {
  constructor(private tenantService: TenantService) {}

  /**
   * Get current tenant info
   */
  @Get("current")
  async getCurrent(@Req() req: Request) {
    return req.tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Create new tenant (platform admin only - add RBAC later)
   */
  @Post()
  async create(@Body() dto: CreateTenantDto) {
    return this.tenantService.create(dto);
  }

  /**
   * Set custom domain for tenant
   */
  @Patch(":id/custom-domain")
  async setCustomDomain(
    @Param("id") id: string,
    @Body() dto: SetCustomDomainDto,
  ) {
    return this.tenantService.setCustomDomain(id, dto.domain);
  }

  /**
   * Verify custom domain (would be called after DNS verification)
   */
  @Post(":id/verify-domain")
  async verifyDomain(@Param("id") id: string) {
    return this.tenantService.verifyCustomDomain(id);
  }
}

  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([Tenant]),
    CacheModule.register({
      ttl: 300000, // 5 minutes default
      max: 1000, // max items in cache
    }),

  controllers: [TenantController],
  exports: [TenantService],

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/tenant/tenant.service.ts
import { Injectable, NotFoundException, Inject } from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { Tenant } from "@repo/database";
import { CACHE_MANAGER } from "@nestjs/cache-manager";
import { type Cache } from "cache-manager";

@Injectable()
export class TenantService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Tenant)
    private tenantRepository: Repository<Tenant>,

    @Inject(CACHE_MANAGER)
    private cacheManager: Cache,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Find tenant by slug (subdomain)
   * Cached for 5 minutes
   */
  async findBySlug(slug: string): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const cacheKey = `tenant:slug:${slug}`;

    // Check cache
    const cached = await this.cacheManager.get<Tenant>(cacheKey);
    if (cached) {
      return cached;
    }

    // Query database
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { slug, active: true },
    });

    // Cache for 5 minutes
    if (tenant) {
      await this.cacheManager.set(cacheKey, tenant, 300000);
    }

    return tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Find tenant by custom domain
   * Cached for 5 minutes
   */
  async findByCustomDomain(domain: string): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const cacheKey = `tenant:domain:${domain}`;

    // Check cache
    const cached = await this.cacheManager.get<Tenant>(cacheKey);
    if (cached) {
      return cached;
    }

    // Query database
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        customDomain: domain,
        customDomainVerified: true,
        active: true,
      },
    });

    // Cache for 5 minutes
    if (tenant) {
      await this.cacheManager.set(cacheKey, tenant, 300000);
    }

    return tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Find tenant by ID
   * Cached for 10 minutes
   */
  async findById(id: string): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const cacheKey = `tenant:id:${id}`;

    const cached = await this.cacheManager.get<Tenant>(cacheKey);
    if (cached) {
      return cached;
    }

    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id, active: true },
    });

    if (tenant) {
      await this.cacheManager.set(cacheKey, tenant, 600000);
    }

    return tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Invalidate all caches for a tenant
   */
  async invalidateCache(tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: tenantId },
    });

    if (!tenant) return;

    await this.cacheManager.del(`tenant:id:${tenantId}`);
    await this.cacheManager.del(`tenant:slug:${tenant.slug}`);

    if (tenant.customDomain) {
      await this.cacheManager.del(`tenant:domain:${tenant.customDomain}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Create tenant (for platform admin)
   */
  async create(data: {
    slug: string;
    name: string;
    environment?: string;
  }): Promise<Tenant> {
    const tenant = this.tenantRepository.create({
      slug: data.slug.toLowerCase(),
      name: data.name,
      environment: data.environment || "production",
      active: true,
    });

    return this.tenantRepository.save(tenant);
  }

  /**
   * Set custom domain
   */
  async setCustomDomain(tenantId: string, domain: string): Promise<Tenant> {
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: tenantId },
    });

    if (!tenant) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Tenant not found");
    }

    tenant.customDomain = domain;
    tenant.customDomainVerified = false; // Require verification

    await this.tenantRepository.save(tenant);
    await this.invalidateCache(tenantId);

    return tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Verify custom domain
   */
  async verifyCustomDomain(tenantId: string): Promise<Tenant> {
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: tenantId },
    });

    if (!tenant) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Tenant not found");
    }

    tenant.customDomainVerified = true;

    await this.tenantRepository.save(tenant);
    await this.invalidateCache(tenantId);

    return tenant;
  }
}

import {
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
  Put,
  Delete,
  Body,
  Param,
  Query,
  UseGuards,
  Request,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiTags,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiBearerAuth,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { UserService } from "./user.service";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";
import { UpdateUserDto, ChangePasswordDto } from "./user.dto";

/**
 * User Controller
 *
 * Handles user management endpoints:
 * - Get user profile
 * - Update user profile
 * - Change password
 * - Get user sessions
 * - Delete account
 */
@ApiTags("Users")
@Controller("users")
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
@ApiBearerAuth()
export class UserController {
  constructor(private readonly userService: UserService) {}

  /**
   * Get current user profile
   */
  @Get("me")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get current user profile" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "User profile retrieved" })
  async getProfile(@Request() req: any) {
    return this.userService.findById(req.user.sub);
  }

  /**
   * Update current user profile
   */
  @Put("me")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Update current user profile" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Profile updated successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Bad request - validation failed" })
  async updateProfile(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() updateUserDto: UpdateUserDto,
  ) {
    await this.userService.update(req.user.sub, updateUserDto);
    return { message: "Profile updated successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Change password
   */
  @Post("me/change-password")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Change user password" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Password changed successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 400,
    description: "Invalid current password or weak new password",
  })
  async changePassword(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() changePasswordDto: ChangePasswordDto,
  ) {
    await this.userService.changePassword(
      req.user.sub,
      changePasswordDto.currentPassword,
      changePasswordDto.newPassword,
    );
    return { message: "Password changed successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Get active sessions for current user
   */
  @Get("me/sessions")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get all active sessions" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Sessions retrieved" })
  async getSessions(@Request() req: any) {
    return this.userService.getActiveSessions(req.user.sub);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke a specific session
   */
  @Delete("me/sessions/:sessionId")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Revoke a specific session" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Session revoked" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "Session not found" })
  async revokeSession(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("sessionId") sessionId: string,
  ) {
    await this.userService.revokeSession(req.user.sub, sessionId);
    return { message: "Session revoked successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all sessions except current
   */
  @Delete("me/sessions")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Revoke all sessions except current" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Sessions revoked" })
  async revokeAllSessions(@Request() req: any) {
    await this.userService.revokeAllSessions(req.user.sub);
    return { message: "All other sessions revoked successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Get security events for current user
   */
  @Get("me/security-events")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get security event history" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Security events retrieved" })
  async getSecurityEvents(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Query("limit") limit: string = "50",
  ) {
    return this.userService.getSecurityEvents(
      req.user.sub,
      parseInt(limit, 10),
    );
  }

  /**
   * Delete account (soft delete)
   */
  @Delete("me")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Delete user account" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Account deleted successfully" })
  async deleteAccount(@Request() req: any, @Body("password") password: string) {
    await this.userService.deleteAccount(req.user.sub, password);
    return { message: "Account deleted successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Get user by ID (admin endpoint - to be protected with role guard later)
   */
  @Get(":id")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get user by ID" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "User retrieved" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "User not found" })
  async getUserById(@Param("id") id: string) {
    return this.userService.findById(id);
  }
}

import {
  IsString,
  IsOptional,
  MinLength,
  MaxLength,
  Matches,
} from "class-validator";
import { ApiProperty } from "@nestjs/swagger";

/**
 * Update User DTO
 */
export class UpdateUserDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "John", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  firstName?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "Doe", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  lastName?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "John Doe", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  displayName?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "+1234567890", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  phone?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "https://example.com/avatar.jpg", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  avatarUrl?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "America/New_York", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  timezone?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "en", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  language?: string;
}

/**
 * Change Password DTO
 */
export class ChangePasswordDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "CurrentP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  currentPassword!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "NewSecureP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(8)
  @MaxLength(128)
  @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[@$!%*?&])[A-Za-z\d@$!%*?&]/, {
    message:
      "Password must contain uppercase, lowercase, number and special character",
  })
  newPassword!: string;
}

import { Module } from "@nestjs/common";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { User, Session, SecurityEvent } from "@repo/database";
import { UserController } from "./user.controller";
import { UserService } from "./user.service";

/**
 * User Module
 *
 * Handles user management functionality:
 * - User profile management
 * - Password changes
 * - Session management
 * - Security event tracking
 * - Account deletion
 */
@Module({
  imports: [TypeOrmModule.forFeature([User, Session, SecurityEvent])],
  controllers: [UserController],
  providers: [UserService],
  exports: [UserService],
})
export class UserModule {}

FILE: apps/auth-service/src/modules/user/user.service.ts
SIZE: 10.22 KB | LINES: 432
