import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  BadRequestException,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, MoreThan } from "typeorm";
import { User, Session, SecurityEvent } from "@repo/database";
import { PasswordService } from "@repo/security";

/**
 * User Service
 *
 * Handles all user management business logic:
 * - User CRUD operations
 * - Password management
 * - Session management
 * - Security event tracking
 */
@Injectable()
export class UserService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private readonly userRepository: Repository<User>,
    @InjectRepository(Session)
    private readonly sessionRepository: Repository<Session>,
    @InjectRepository(SecurityEvent)
    private readonly securityEventRepository: Repository<SecurityEvent>,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Find user by ID
   */
  async findById(id: string): Promise<User> {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id },
      relations: ["tenant"],
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    return user;
  }

  /**
   * Find user by email
   */
  async findByEmail(email: string, tenantId?: string): Promise<User | null> {
    const where: any = { email };
    if (tenantId) {
      where.tenantId = tenantId;
    }

    return this.userRepository.findOne({
      where,
      relations: ["tenant", "mfaSettings"],
    });
  }

  /**
   * Update user profile
   */
  async update(
    userId: string,
    data: {
      firstName?: string;
      lastName?: string;
      displayName?: string;
      phone?: string;
      avatarUrl?: string;
      timezone?: string;
      language?: string;
    },
  ) {
    const user = await this.findById(userId);

    Object.assign(user, data);
    await this.userRepository.save(user);

    // Log profile update
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "USER_UPDATED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
      metadata: { fields: Object.keys(data) },
    });

    return user;
  }

  /**
   * Change password
   */
  async changePassword(
    userId: string,
    currentPassword: string,
    newPassword: string,
  ) {
    // Get user with password hash
    const user = await this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHash")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHistory")
      .where("user.id = :id", { id: userId })
      .getOne();

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Verify current password
    const isValid = await PasswordService.verify(
      user.passwordHash,
      currentPassword,
    );

    if (!isValid) {
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "PASSWORD_CHANGE_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "system",
        metadata: { reason: "invalid_current_password" },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Current password is incorrect");
    }

    // Validate new password strength
    const validation = PasswordService.validateStrength(newPassword);
    if (!validation.isValid) {
      throw new BadRequestException({
        message: "New password does not meet security requirements",
        errors: validation.errors,
        score: validation.score,
      });
    }

    // Check password history
    const passwordHistory = user.passwordHistory || [];
    for (const oldHash of passwordHistory) {
      const isReused = await PasswordService.verify(oldHash, newPassword);
      if (isReused) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          "Password has been used recently. Please choose a different password.",
        );
      }
    }

    // Hash new password
    const newHash = await PasswordService.hash(newPassword);

    // Update password and history
    const updatedHistory = [user.passwordHash, ...passwordHistory].slice(0, 5);

    await this.userRepository.update(userId, {
      passwordHash: newHash,
      passwordHistory: updatedHistory,
      passwordChangedAt: new Date(),
      forcePasswordChange: false,
    });

    // Log password change
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "PASSWORD_CHANGED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });

    // Revoke all sessions except current (force re-login on other devices)
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { userId, isActive: true },
      {
        isActive: false,
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "password_changed",
      },
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get active sessions for user
   */
  async getActiveSessions(userId: string) {
    const sessions = await this.sessionRepository.find({
      where: {
        userId,
        isActive: true,
        expiresAt: MoreThan(new Date()),
      },
      order: {
        lastActivity: "DESC",
      },
    });

    return sessions.map((session) => ({
      id: session.id,
      deviceInfo: session.deviceInfo,
      ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
      location: session.location,
      lastActivity: session.lastActivity,
      createdAt: session.createdAt,
    }));
  }

  /**
   * Revoke a specific session
   */
  async revokeSession(userId: string, sessionId: string) {
    const session = await this.sessionRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: sessionId, userId },
    });

    if (!session) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Session not found");
    }

    await this.sessionRepository.update(sessionId, {
      isActive: false,
      revokedAt: new Date(),
      revokedReason: "manual_revocation",
    });

    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      eventType: "SESSION_REVOKED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
      metadata: { sessionId },
    });
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all sessions for user
   */
  async revokeAllSessions(userId: string) {
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { userId, isActive: true },
      {
        isActive: false,
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "user_request",
      },
    );

    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      eventType: "ALL_SESSIONS_REVOKED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Get security events for user
   */
  async getSecurityEvents(userId: string, limit: number = 50) {
    const events = await this.securityEventRepository.find({
      where: { userId },
      order: { createdAt: "DESC" },
      take: limit,
    });

    return events.map((event) => ({
      id: event.id,
      eventType: event.eventType,
      severity: event.severity,
      ipAddress: event.ipAddress,
      location: event.location,
      metadata: event.metadata,
      createdAt: event.createdAt,
    }));
  }

  /**
   * Delete account (soft delete)
   */
  async deleteAccount(userId: string, password: string) {
    // Get user with password hash
    const user = await this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHash")
      .where("user.id = :id", { id: userId })
      .getOne();

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Verify password
    const isValid = await PasswordService.verify(user.passwordHash, password);

    if (!isValid) {
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "ACCOUNT_DELETION_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "system",
        metadata: { reason: "invalid_password" },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Password is incorrect");
    }

    // Soft delete user
    await this.userRepository.softDelete(userId);

    // Revoke all sessions
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { userId, isActive: true },
      {
        isActive: false,
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "account_deleted",
      },
    );

    // Log account deletion
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "ACCOUNT_DELETED",
      severity: "critical",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Check if account is locked
   */
  async isAccountLocked(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.findById(userId);

    if (!user.lockedUntil) {
      return false;
    }

    return user.lockedUntil > new Date();
  }

  /**
   * Increment failed login attempts
   */
  async incrementFailedAttempts(userId: string) {
    const user = await this.findById(userId);

    const newAttempts = user.failedLoginAttempts + 1;

    const update: any = {
      failedLoginAttempts: newAttempts,
    };

    // Lock account after 5 failed attempts
    if (newAttempts >= 5) {
      update.lockedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000); // 15 minutes

      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "ACCOUNT_LOCKED",
        severity: "critical",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "system",
        metadata: { attempts: newAttempts },
      });
    }

    await this.userRepository.update(userId, update);
  }

  /**
   * Reset failed login attempts
   */
  async resetFailedAttempts(userId: string) {
    await this.userRepository.update(userId, {
      failedLoginAttempts: 0,
      lockedUntil: undefined,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Update last login timestamp
   */
  async updateLastLogin(userId: string, ip: string) {
    await this.userRepository.update(userId, {
      lastLoginAt: new Date(),
      lastLoginIp: ip,
      failedLoginAttempts: 0,
      lockedUntil: undefined,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Log security event
   */
  private async logSecurityEvent(data: {
    userId?: string;
    tenantId?: string;
    eventType: string;
    severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
    ipAddress: string;
    userAgent: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  }) {
    await this.securityEventRepository.save({
      userId: data.userId,
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      eventType: data.eventType,
      severity: data.severity,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      metadata: data.metadata || {},
    });
  }
}

This is a [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) project bootstrapped with [`create-next-app`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/create-next-app).

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash
npm run dev
# or
yarn dev
# or
pnpm dev
# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load Inter, a custom Google Font.

## Learn More

To learn more about Next.js, take a look at the following resources:

- [Next.js Documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs) - learn about Next.js features and API.
- [Learn Next.js](https://nextjs.org/learn) - an interactive Next.js tutorial.

You can check out [the Next.js GitHub repository](https://github.com/vercel/next.js) - your feedback and contributions are welcome!

## Deploy on Vercel

The easiest way to deploy your Next.js app is to use the [Vercel Platform](https://vercel.com/new?utm_medium=default-template&filter=next.js&utm_source=create-next-app&utm_campaign=create-next-app-readme) from the creators of Next.js.

Check out our [Next.js deployment documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/deploying) for more details.

FILE: consolidate.js
SIZE: 9.59 KB | LINES: 379
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

/**
 * Code Consolidator Script
 * Recursively scans directories and consolidates all code files into a single text file
 */

class CodeConsolidator {
  constructor() {
    // Common code file extensions
    this.codeExtensions = new Set([
      ".js",
      ".jsx",
      ".ts",
      ".tsx",
      ".py",
      ".java",
      ".c",
      ".cpp",
      ".cc",
      ".cxx",
      ".cs",
      ".php",
      ".rb",
      ".go",
      ".rs",
      ".swift",
      ".kt",
      ".scala",
      ".r",
      ".m",
      ".mm",
      ".html",
      ".htm",
      ".css",
      ".scss",
      ".sass",
      ".less",
      ".xml",
      ".json",
      ".yaml",
      ".yml",
      ".toml",
      ".sh",
      ".bash",
      ".zsh",
      ".fish",
      ".ps1",
      ".bat",
      ".sql",
      ".graphql",
      ".proto",
      ".thrift",
      ".dockerfile",
      ".makefile",
      ".cmake",
      ".vue",
      ".svelte",
      ".dart",
      ".lua",
      ".pl",
      ".pm",
    ]);

    // Files to exclude
    this.excludeFiles = new Set([
      "package-lock.json",
      "yarn.lock",
      ".DS_Store",
      "Thumbs.db",
      ".env",
      ".env.local",
      ".env.production",
      ".env.development",
    ]);

    // Directories to exclude
    this.excludeDirs = new Set([
      "node_modules",
      ".git",
      ".svn",
      ".hg",
      "dist",
      "build",
      "target",
      "bin",
      "obj",
      "__pycache__",
      ".pytest_cache",
      "venv",
      "env",
      ".venv",
      ".env",
      "vendor",
      ".idea",
      ".vscode",
      ".vs",
      "coverage",
      ".nyc_output",
      ".cache",
    ]);

    this.stats = {
      filesProcessed: 0,
      totalLines: 0,
      skippedFiles: 0,
      errors: 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Check if file should be processed based on extension
   */
  isCodeFile(filePath) {
    const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
    const basename = path.basename(filePath).toLowerCase();

    // Special files without extensions
    const specialFiles = [
      "dockerfile",
      "makefile",
      "jenkinsfile",
      "readme",
      "license",
      "changelog",
    ];
    if (specialFiles.some((name) => basename.includes(name))) {
      return true;
    }

    return this.codeExtensions.has(ext);
  }

  /**
   * Check if file/directory should be excluded
   */
  shouldExclude(itemPath, isDirectory = false) {
    const basename = path.basename(itemPath);

    if (isDirectory) {
      return this.excludeDirs.has(basename) || basename.startsWith(".");
    }

    return (
      this.excludeFiles.has(basename) ||
      (basename.startsWith(".") && !this.isCodeFile(itemPath))
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get file size in a readable format
   */
  getFileSize(filePath) {
    try {
      const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
      const bytes = stats.size;

      if (bytes === 0) return "0 B";
      const k = 1024;
      const sizes = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];
      const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
      return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + " " + sizes[i];
    } catch (error) {
      return "Unknown";
    }
  }

  /**
   * Process a single file and add its content to the output
   */
  processFile(filePath, outputStream) {
    try {
      const relativePath = path.relative(process.cwd(), filePath);
      const fileSize = this.getFileSize(filePath);
      const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf-8");
      const lineCount = content.split("\n").length;

      // Write file header
      outputStream.write("\n" + "=".repeat(80) + "\n");
      outputStream.write(`FILE: ${relativePath}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`SIZE: ${fileSize} | LINES: ${lineCount}\n`);
      outputStream.write("=".repeat(80) + "\n\n");

      // Write file content
      outputStream.write(content);
      outputStream.write("\n\n");

      this.stats.filesProcessed++;
      this.stats.totalLines += lineCount;

      console.log(`✓ Processed: ${relativePath} (${lineCount} lines)`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`✗ Error processing ${filePath}: ${error.message}`);
      this.stats.errors++;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Recursively scan directory and process all code files
   */
  scanDirectory(dirPath, outputStream) {
    try {
      const items = fs.readdirSync(dirPath);

      for (const item of items) {
        const itemPath = path.join(dirPath, item);
        const stats = fs.statSync(itemPath);

        if (stats.isDirectory()) {
          if (!this.shouldExclude(itemPath, true)) {
            this.scanDirectory(itemPath, outputStream);
          } else {
            console.log(
              `⏭ Skipping directory: ${path.relative(process.cwd(), itemPath)}`,
            );
          }
        } else if (stats.isFile()) {
          if (
            this.isCodeFile(itemPath) &&
            !this.shouldExclude(itemPath, false)
          ) {
            this.processFile(itemPath, outputStream);
          } else {
            this.stats.skippedFiles++;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`✗ Error scanning directory ${dirPath}: ${error.message}`);
      this.stats.errors++;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Main consolidation function
   */
  consolidate(inputPath = ".", outputPath = "consolidated-code.txt") {
    console.log("🚀 Starting code consolidation...\n");
    console.log(`Input: ${path.resolve(inputPath)}`);
    console.log(`Output: ${path.resolve(outputPath)}\n`);

    const outputStream = fs.createWriteStream(outputPath);

    // Write header
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    outputStream.write(`CODE CONSOLIDATION REPORT\n`);
    outputStream.write(`Generated: ${timestamp}\n`);
    outputStream.write(`Input Directory: ${path.resolve(inputPath)}\n`);
    outputStream.write(`${"=".repeat(80)}\n\n`);

    try {
      const inputStats = fs.statSync(inputPath);

      if (inputStats.isDirectory()) {
        this.scanDirectory(inputPath, outputStream);
      } else if (inputStats.isFile()) {
        this.processFile(inputPath, outputStream);
      } else {
        throw new Error("Input path is neither a file nor a directory");
      }

      // Write footer with statistics
      outputStream.write("\n" + "=".repeat(80) + "\n");
      outputStream.write("CONSOLIDATION SUMMARY\n");
      outputStream.write("=".repeat(80) + "\n");
      outputStream.write(`Files Processed: ${this.stats.filesProcessed}\n`);
      outputStream.write(
        `Total Lines: ${this.stats.totalLines.toLocaleString()}\n`,
      );
      outputStream.write(`Files Skipped: ${this.stats.skippedFiles}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`Errors: ${this.stats.errors}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`Generated: ${timestamp}\n`);

      outputStream.end();

      console.log("\n✅ Consolidation completed successfully!");
      console.log(`📊 Statistics:`);
      console.log(`   • Files processed: ${this.stats.filesProcessed}`);
      console.log(
        `   • Total lines: ${this.stats.totalLines.toLocaleString()}`,
      );
      console.log(`   • Files skipped: ${this.stats.skippedFiles}`);
      console.log(`   • Errors: ${this.stats.errors}`);
      console.log(`📁 Output saved to: ${path.resolve(outputPath)}`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`💥 Fatal error: ${error.message}`);
      outputStream.destroy();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Add custom file extensions
   */
  addExtensions(extensions) {
    extensions.forEach((ext) => {
      if (!ext.startsWith(".")) ext = "." + ext;
      this.codeExtensions.add(ext.toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  /**
   * Add custom exclude patterns
   */
  addExcludes(files = [], dirs = []) {
    files.forEach((file) => this.excludeFiles.add(file));
    dirs.forEach((dir) => this.excludeDirs.add(dir));
  }
}

// CLI Usage
if (require.main === module) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const consolidator = new CodeConsolidator();

  // Parse command line arguments
  let inputPath = ".";
  let outputPath = "consolidated-code.txt";
  let customExtensions = [];
  let excludeFiles = [];
  let excludeDirs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    switch (args[i]) {
      case "--input":
      case "-i":
        inputPath = args[++i];
        break;
      case "--output":
      case "-o":
        outputPath = args[++i];
        break;
      case "--extensions":
      case "-e":
        customExtensions = args[++i].split(",");
        break;
      case "--exclude-files":
        excludeFiles = args[++i].split(",");
        break;
      case "--exclude-dirs":
        excludeDirs = args[++i].split(",");
        break;
      case "--help":
      case "-h":
        console.log(`
Code Consolidator - Merge all code files into one text file

Usage: node consolidate.js [options]

Options:
  -i, --input <path>          Input directory or file (default: current directory)
  -o, --output <path>         Output file path (default: consolidated-code.txt)
  -e, --extensions <list>     Additional file extensions (comma-separated)
  --exclude-files <list>      Additional files to exclude (comma-separated)
  --exclude-dirs <list>       Additional directories to exclude (comma-separated)
  -h, --help                  Show this help

Examples:
  node consolidate.js
  node consolidate.js -i ./src -o output.txt
  node consolidate.js -e "md,txt" --exclude-dirs "temp,cache"
                `);
        process.exit(0);
    }
  }

  // Apply custom settings
  if (customExtensions.length) consolidator.addExtensions(customExtensions);
  if (excludeFiles.length || excludeDirs.length)
    consolidator.addExcludes(excludeFiles, excludeDirs);

  // Run consolidation
  consolidator.consolidate(inputPath, outputPath);
}

module.exports = CodeConsolidator;

# @repo/database

**Fort Knox Database Package** - TypeORM entities and migrations for the authentication system.

## 📦 What's Included

### Entities (7 Core Tables)

1. **Tenant** - Multi-tenant organizations
2. **User** - User accounts with comprehensive security
3. **Session** - Active sessions with device tracking
4. **MfaSettings** - Multi-factor authentication settings
5. **SecurityEvent** - Security audit log
6. **Role** - RBAC roles
7. **Permission** - RBAC permissions

### Features

✅ **Multi-Tenancy** - Complete tenant isolation  
✅ **Soft Deletes** - All entities support soft deletion  
✅ **Timestamps** - Auto-managed created/updated dates  
✅ **UUID Primary Keys** - Better for distributed systems  
✅ **JSONB Fields** - Flexible metadata storage  
✅ **Comprehensive Indexes** - Optimized for performance  
✅ **Foreign Key Constraints** - Data integrity  
✅ **Database Comments** - Self-documenting schema

---

## 🚀 Quick Start

### 1. Installation

```bash
cd packages/database
npm install
```

### 2. Environment Variables

Create `.env` file in your auth-service:

```env
DATABASE_HOST=localhost
DATABASE_PORT=5432
DATABASE_USER=fortknox
DATABASE_PASSWORD=fortknox_dev_password
DATABASE_NAME=fortknox_auth
DATABASE_LOGGING=true
DATABASE_POOL_MAX=20
DATABASE_POOL_MIN=2
```

### 3. Run Migration

```bash
# Build the package first
npm run build

# Run migration
npm run migration:run
```

---

## 📚 Usage

### In Your NestJS Application

#### app.module.ts

```typescript
import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { dataSourceOptions } from '@repo/database';

@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forRoot(dataSourceOptions),
    // ... other modules
  ],
})
export class AppModule {}
```

#### Using Entities in Modules

```typescript
import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { User, Tenant } from '@repo/database';
import { UserService } from './user.service';

@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([User, Tenant]),
  ],
  providers: [UserService],
  exports: [UserService],
})
export class UserModule {}
```

#### Using in Services

```typescript
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
import { User } from '@repo/database';

@Injectable()
export class UserService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private readonly userRepository: Repository<User>,
  ) {}

  async findByEmail(email: string, tenantId: string): Promise<User | null> {
    return this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { email, tenantId },
      relations: ['tenant', 'mfaSettings'],
    });
  }

  async create(userData: Partial<User>): Promise<User> {
    const user = this.userRepository.create(userData);
    return this.userRepository.save(user);
  }

  async updateLastLogin(userId: string, ip: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.userRepository.update(userId, {
      lastLoginAt: new Date(),
      lastLoginIp: ip,
      failedLoginAttempts: 0,
    });
  }
}
```

## 🗄️ Database Schema

### Entity Relationships

```
Tenant (1) ──── (M) User
                  │
                  ├─── (1) MfaSettings
                  ├─── (M) Session
                  └─── (M) SecurityEvent

Tenant (1) ──── (M) Role ──── (M) Permission
```

### Key Tables

#### Tenants

```sql
tenants (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  name varchar(255),
  subdomain varchar(100) UNIQUE,
  custom_domain varchar(255),
  status enum('active', 'trial', 'suspended', 'cancelled'),
  plan enum('free', 'starter', 'professional', 'enterprise'),
  settings jsonb,
  ...
)
```

#### Users

```sql
users (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id uuid REFERENCES tenants(id),
  email varchar(255),
  password_hash varchar(255),
  email_verified boolean,
  status enum('active', 'inactive', 'suspended', 'deleted'),
  password_history jsonb,
  failed_login_attempts int,
  locked_until timestamp,
  ...
)
```

#### Sessions

```sql
sessions (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id),
  token_hash varchar(255) UNIQUE,
  device_info jsonb,
  ip_address varchar(45),
  expires_at timestamp,
  ...
)
```

```bash
# Generate a new migration (after entity changes)
npm run migration:generate -- src/migrations/MyMigration

# Create an empty migration
npm run migration:create -- src/migrations/MyMigration

# Run pending migrations
npm run migration:run

# Revert last migration
npm run migration:revert

# Show migration status
npm run migration:show

# Sync schema (DEVELOPMENT ONLY!)
npm run schema:sync

# Drop all tables (DANGER!)
npm run schema:drop
```

## 🎯 Common Queries

### Find User with Relations

```typescript
const user = await userRepository.findOne({
  where: { id: userId },
  relations: ['tenant', 'mfaSettings', 'sessions'],
});
```

### Create User with MFA Settings

```typescript
const user = userRepository.create({
  email: 'user@example.com',
  passwordHash: hashedPassword,
  tenantId: tenant.id,
  mfaSettings: {
    totpEnabled: false,
    backupCodes: [],
  },
});
await userRepository.save(user);
```

### Get Active Sessions for User

```typescript
const activeSessions = await sessionRepository.find({
  where: {
    userId,
    isActive: true,
    expiresAt: MoreThan(new Date()),
  },
  order: {
    lastActivity: 'DESC',
  },
});
```

### Log Security Event

```typescript
await securityEventRepository.save({
  userId,
  tenantId,
  eventType: 'LOGIN_SUCCESS',
  severity: 'info',
  ipAddress: req.ip,
  userAgent: req.headers['user-agent'],
  metadata: { method: 'password' },
});
```

### Get User's Permissions

```typescript
const user = await userRepository.findOne({
  where: { id: userId },
  relations: ['roles', 'roles.permissions'],
});

const permissions = user.roles
  .flatMap((role) => role.permissions)
  .map((perm) => `${perm.resource}:${perm.action}`);
```

---

## 🔒 Security Features

### Password History

The `password_history` JSONB field stores the last 5 password hashes to prevent reuse:

```typescript
// Check password history
const isReused = await Promise.all(
  user.passwordHistory.map((oldHash) => 
    PasswordService.verify(oldHash, newPassword)
  )
).then((results) => results.some((r) => r));

if (isReused) {
  throw new Error('Password has been used recently');
}

// Update password with history
user.passwordHistory = [
  user.passwordHash,
  ...user.passwordHistory,
].slice(0, 5);
user.passwordHash = newHash;
```

### Account Lockout

```typescript
// Increment failed attempts
user.failedLoginAttempts += 1;

if (user.failedLoginAttempts >= 5) {
  // Lock for 15 minutes
  user.lockedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000);
}

await userRepository.save(user);
```

### Session Expiration

Sessions automatically expire based on `expires_at`:

```typescript
// Check if session is valid
const isValid = 
  session.isActive && 
  !session.revokedAt && 
  session.expiresAt > new Date();
```

---

## 📊 Performance Tips

### Indexes

All critical fields are indexed:
- User email + tenantId (unique)
- Session tokenHash (unique)
- All foreign keys
- Frequently queried fields (status, email_verified, etc.)

### Query Optimization

```typescript
// ✅ Good - Select only needed fields
const user = await userRepository.findOne({
  where: { id: userId },
  select: ['id', 'email', 'status'],
});

// ✅ Good - Use query builder for complex queries
const users = await userRepository
  .createQueryBuilder('user')
  .where('user.tenant_id = :tenantId', { tenantId })
  .andWhere('user.status = :status', { status: 'active' })
  .andWhere('user.last_login_at > :date', { 
    date: new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) 
  })
  .getMany();

// ❌ Bad - Loading all relations unnecessarily
const user = await userRepository.findOne({
  where: { id: userId },
  relations: ['tenant', 'sessions', 'mfaSettings', 'securityEvents'],
});
```

## 🧪 Testing

### Example Test Setup

```typescript
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { User, dataSourceOptions } from '@repo/database';

describe('UserService', () => {
  let userRepository: Repository<User>;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    const module = await Test.createTestingModule({
      imports: [
        TypeOrmModule.forRoot({
          ...dataSourceOptions,
          database: 'fortknox_test',
          synchronize: true, // OK for tests
        }),
        TypeOrmModule.forFeature([User]),
      ],
    }).compile();

    userRepository = module.get('UserRepository');
  });

  it('should create user', async () => {
    const user = await userRepository.save({
      email: 'test@example.com',
      passwordHash: 'hashed',
      tenantId: 'tenant-id',
    });

    expect(user.id).toBeDefined();
    expect(user.email).toBe('test@example.com');
  });
});
```

---

## 🚨 Important Notes

### DO NOT Use in Production:
- ❌ `synchronize: true`
- ❌ `dropSchema: true`
- ❌ Hardcoded credentials

### ALWAYS Use:
- ✅ Migrations for schema changes
- ✅ Environment variables for config
- ✅ Connection pooling
- ✅ Query parameter binding (prevents SQL injection)

---

## 📝 Entity Field Reference

### User Entity

| Field | Type | Description |
|-------|------|-------------|
| `email` | varchar(255) | User email (unique per tenant) |
| `passwordHash` | varchar(255) | Argon2id password hash |
| `passwordHistory` | jsonb | Last 5 password hashes |
| `status` | enum | active, inactive, suspended, deleted |
| `emailVerified` | boolean | Email verification status |
| `failedLoginAttempts` | int | Failed login counter |
| `lockedUntil` | timestamp | Account lockout expiration |
| `lastLoginAt` | timestamp | Last successful login |
| `metadata` | jsonb | Additional user data |

### Session Entity

| Field | Type | Description |
|-------|------|-------------|
| `tokenHash` | varchar(255) | Hashed session token (unique) |
| `deviceInfo` | jsonb | Browser, OS, device fingerprint |
| `ipAddress` | varchar(45) | IP address |
| `expiresAt` | timestamp | Session expiration |
| `lastActivity` | timestamp | Last activity timestamp |
| `revokedAt` | timestamp | Manual revocation time |

When adding new entities:

1. Create entity in `src/entities/`
2. Export from `src/entities/index.ts`
3. Generate migration: `npm run migration:generate`
4. Test migration: `npm run migration:run`
5. Update this README

import "reflect-metadata";
import { DataSource, DataSourceOptions } from "typeorm";
import * as path from "path";

// Import all entities explicitly
import { Tenant } from "./entities/tenant.entity";
import { User } from "./entities/user.entity";
import { Session } from "./entities/session.entity";
import { MfaSettings } from "./entities/mfa-settings.entity";
import { SecurityEvent } from "./entities/security-event.entity";
import { Role } from "./entities/role.entity";
import { Permission } from "./entities/permission.entity";

/**
 * TypeORM DataSource Configuration
 *
 * This file is used by TypeORM CLI for migrations
 * and can also be imported by the NestJS application
 */

const isProduction = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production";
const useSSL = process.env.DB_SSL === "true";

export const dataSourceOptions: DataSourceOptions = {
  type: "postgres",
  host: process.env.DATABASE_HOST || "localhost",
  port: parseInt(process.env.DATABASE_PORT || "5432", 10),
  username: process.env.DATABASE_USER || "postgres",
  password: process.env.DATABASE_PASSWORD || "",
  database: process.env.DATABASE_NAME || "postgres",

  // Entities - explicit import for reliability
  entities: [
    Tenant,
    User,
    Session,
    MfaSettings,
    SecurityEvent,
    Role,
    Permission,
  ],

  // Migrations
  migrations: [path.join(__dirname, "migrations", "*.{ts,js}")],

  // Migration settings
  migrationsTableName: "migrations",
  migrationsRun: false, // Don't auto-run migrations

  // Logging
  logging: process.env.DATABASE_LOGGING === "true",
  logger: "advanced-console",

  // Connection pool
  extra: {
    max: parseInt(process.env.DATABASE_POOL_MAX || "20", 10),
    min: parseInt(process.env.DATABASE_POOL_MIN || "2", 10),
    idleTimeoutMillis: 30000,
    connectionTimeoutMillis: 2000,
  },

  // SSL (for production or cloud databases like Supabase)
  ssl: useSSL
    ? {
        rejectUnauthorized: false, // Supabase requires this
      }
    : false,

  // Schema
  schema: process.env.DATABASE_SCHEMA || "public",

  // Synchronize (NEVER use in production!)
  synchronize: false,
};

// Create and export DataSource
const AppDataSource = new DataSource(dataSourceOptions);

export default AppDataSource;

import {
  PrimaryGeneratedColumn,
  CreateDateColumn,
  UpdateDateColumn,
  DeleteDateColumn,
  BaseEntity as TypeORMBaseEntity,
} from "typeorm";

/**
 * Base Entity
 *
 * All entities extend this to get common fields:
 * - id (UUID primary key)
 * - createdAt (auto-generated timestamp)
 * - updatedAt (auto-updated timestamp)
 * - deletedAt (soft delete timestamp)
 */
export abstract class BaseEntity extends TypeORMBaseEntity {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn("uuid")
  id!: string;

  @CreateDateColumn({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    name: "created_at",
  })
  createdAt!: Date;

  @UpdateDateColumn({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    name: "updated_at",
  })
  updatedAt!: Date;

  @DeleteDateColumn({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    name: "deleted_at",
    nullable: true,
  })
  deletedAt?: Date;
}

FILE: packages/database/src/entities/mfa-settings.entity.ts
SIZE: 2.94 KB | LINES: 153

import { Entity, Column, OneToOne, JoinColumn } from "typeorm";
import { BaseEntity } from "./base.entity";
import { User } from "./user.entity";

/**
 * MFA Settings Entity
 *
 * Manages multi-factor authentication settings for users:
 * - TOTP (Google Authenticator)
 * - SMS OTP
 * - Email OTP
 * - WebAuthn/FIDO2
 * - Backup codes
 */
@Entity("mfa_settings")
export class MfaSettings extends BaseEntity {
  @Column({ type: "uuid", name: "user_id", unique: true })
  userId!: string;

  @OneToOne(() => User, (user) => user.mfaSettings, {
    onDelete: "CASCADE",
  })
  @JoinColumn({ name: "user_id" })
  user!: User;

  // TOTP Settings
  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "totp_enabled",
  })
  totpEnabled!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "text",
    nullable: true,
    name: "totp_secret",
    select: false,
    comment: "Encrypted TOTP secret",
  })
  totpSecret?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "totp_verified_at",
  })
  totpVerifiedAt?: Date;

  // SMS Settings
  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "sms_enabled",
  })
  smsEnabled!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 50,
    nullable: true,
    name: "sms_phone",
  })
  smsPhone?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "sms_verified_at",
  })
  smsVerifiedAt?: Date;

  // Email Settings
  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "email_enabled",
  })
  emailEnabled!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "email_verified_at",
  })
  emailVerifiedAt?: Date;

  // WebAuthn Settings
  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "webauthn_enabled",
  })
  webauthnEnabled!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    default: [],
    name: "webauthn_credentials",
    comment: "Registered WebAuthn/FIDO2 credentials",
  })
  webauthnCredentials!: Array<{
    id: string;
    publicKey: string;
    counter: number;
    deviceName?: string;
    createdAt: Date;
  }>;

  // Backup Codes
  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    default: [],
    name: "backup_codes",
    select: false,
    comment: "Hashed backup codes",
  })
  backupCodes!: string[];

  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    default: [],
    name: "backup_codes_used",
    comment: "Used backup codes (hashed)",
  })
  backupCodesUsed!: string[];

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "backup_codes_generated_at",
  })
  backupCodesGeneratedAt?: Date;

  // MFA Enforcement
  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "mfa_required",
    comment: "Whether MFA is required for this user",
  })
  mfaRequired!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 50,
    nullable: true,
    name: "preferred_method",
    comment: "Preferred MFA method (totp, sms, email, webauthn)",
  })
  preferredMethod?: "totp" | "sms" | "email" | "webauthn";
}

import { Entity, Column, Index, ManyToMany } from "typeorm";
import { BaseEntity } from "./base.entity";
import { Role } from "./role.entity";

/**
 * Permission Entity
 *
 * Defines granular permissions in the system:
 * - Resource-based permissions (user, tenant, role, etc.)
 * - Action-based permissions (create, read, update, delete)
 */
@Entity("permissions")
@Index(["resource", "action"], { unique: true })
@Index(["resource"])
export class Permission extends BaseEntity {
  @Column({ type: "varchar", length: 100 })
  resource!: string;

  @Column({ type: "varchar", length: 50 })
  action!: string;

  @Column({ type: "text", nullable: true })
  description?: string;

  @ManyToMany(() => Role, (role: any) => role.permissions)
  roles!: Role[];
}

import {
  Entity,
  Column,
  Index,
  ManyToOne,
  ManyToMany,
  JoinColumn,
  JoinTable,
} from "typeorm";
import { BaseEntity } from "./base.entity";
import { Tenant } from "./tenant.entity";
import { Permission } from "./permission.entity";

/**
 * Role Entity
 *
 * Defines roles within the RBAC system:
 * - System roles (cannot be deleted)
 * - Custom tenant roles
 * - Hierarchical roles (role inheritance)
 */
@Entity("roles")
@Index(["tenantId", "name"], { unique: true })
@Index(["tenantId"])
@Index(["isSystem"])
export class Role extends BaseEntity {
  @Column({ type: "uuid", name: "tenant_id" })
  tenantId!: string;

  @ManyToOne(() => Tenant, {
    onDelete: "CASCADE",
  })
  @JoinColumn({ name: "tenant_id" })
  tenant!: Tenant;

  @Column({ type: "varchar", length: 100 })
  name!: string;

  @Column({ type: "text", nullable: true })
  description?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "is_system",
    comment: "System roles cannot be modified or deleted",
  })
  isSystem!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "uuid",
    nullable: true,
    name: "parent_role_id",
    comment: "Parent role for inheritance",
  })
  parentRoleId?: string;

  @ManyToOne(() => Role, {
    onDelete: "SET NULL",
    nullable: true,
  })
  @JoinColumn({ name: "parent_role_id" })
  parentRole?: Role;

  @ManyToMany(() => Permission, (permission) => permission.roles, {
    cascade: true,
  })
  @JoinTable({
    name: "role_permissions",
    joinColumn: { name: "role_id", referencedColumnName: "id" },
    inverseJoinColumn: { name: "permission_id", referencedColumnName: "id" },
  })
  permissions!: Permission[];
}

import { Entity, Column, Index, ManyToOne, JoinColumn } from "typeorm";
import { BaseEntity } from "./base.entity";
import { User } from "./user.entity";

/**
 * Session Entity
 *
 * Tracks active user sessions with:
 * - Device fingerprinting
 * - IP and location tracking
 * - Activity monitoring
 * - Automatic expiration
 */
@Entity("sessions")
@Index(["userId"])
@Index(["tokenHash"], { unique: true })
@Index(["expiresAt"])
export class Session extends BaseEntity {
  @Column({ type: "uuid", name: "user_id" })
  userId!: string;

  @ManyToOne(() => User, (user) => user.sessions, {
    onDelete: "CASCADE",
  })
  @JoinColumn({ name: "user_id" })
  user!: User;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    unique: true,
    name: "token_hash",
    comment: "Hashed session token",
  })
  tokenHash!: string;

  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    name: "device_info",
    comment: "Device information (browser, OS, etc.)",
  })
  deviceInfo!: {
    browser?: string;
    os?: string;
    device?: string;
    fingerprint: string;
  };

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 45,
    name: "ip_address",
  })
  ipAddress!: string;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    comment: "Geographic location (city, country)",
  })
  location?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "text",
    name: "user_agent",
  })
  userAgent!: string;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    name: "last_activity",
    default: () => "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP",
  })
  lastActivity!: Date;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    name: "expires_at",
  })
  expiresAt!: Date;

  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: true,
    name: "is_active",
  })
  isActive!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    name: "refresh_token_hash",
    comment: "Hashed refresh token",
  })
  refreshTokenHash?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "revoked_at",
  })
  revokedAt?: Date;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    name: "revoked_reason",
  })
  revokedReason?: string;
}

// packages/database/src/entities/tenant.entity.ts
import { Entity, Column, OneToMany, Index } from "typeorm";
import { BaseEntity } from "./base.entity";
import { User } from "./user.entity";

@Entity("tenants")
@Index(["slug"], { unique: true })
@Index(["customDomain"], { unique: true, where: "custom_domain IS NOT NULL" })
export class Tenant extends BaseEntity {
  @Column({ type: "varchar", length: 100, unique: true })
  slug!: string; // acme, startup, corp

  @Column({ type: "varchar", length: 255 })
  name!: string; // Acme Corp

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    unique: true,
    name: "custom_domain",
  })
  customDomain?: string; // auth.acme.com

  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "custom_domain_verified",
  })
  customDomainVerified!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 100,
    default: "production",
  })
  environment!: string; // production, staging, development

  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    nullable: true,
    name: "feature_flags",
  })
  featureFlags?: Record<string, boolean>;

  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    nullable: true,
  })
  settings?: {
    allowSignup?: boolean;
    requireEmailVerification?: boolean;
    sessionTimeout?: number;
    mfaRequired?: boolean;
  };

  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: true,
  })
  active!: boolean;

  @OneToMany(() => User, (user) => user.tenant)
  users!: User[];
}

import {
  Entity,
  Column,
  Index,
  ManyToOne,
  OneToOne,
  OneToMany,
  JoinColumn,
} from "typeorm";
import { BaseEntity } from "./base.entity";
import { Tenant } from "./tenant.entity";
import { MfaSettings } from "./mfa-settings.entity";
import { Session } from "./session.entity";

/**
 * User Entity
 *
 * Core user entity with comprehensive security features:
 * - Argon2id password hashing
 * - Email/phone verification
 * - Password history
 * - Account lockout
 * - Multi-tenant support
 */
@Entity("users")
@Index(["email", "tenantId"], { unique: true })
@Index(["tenantId"])
@Index(["status"])
@Index(["emailVerified"])
@Index(["lastLoginAt"])
export class User extends BaseEntity {
  // Tenant relation
  @Column({ type: "uuid", name: "tenant_id" })
  tenantId!: string;

  @ManyToOne(() => Tenant, (tenant) => tenant.users, {
    onDelete: "CASCADE",
  })
  @JoinColumn({ name: "tenant_id" })
  tenant!: Tenant;

  // Basic Information
  @Column({ type: "varchar", length: 255 })
  email!: string;

  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "email_verified",
  })
  emailVerified!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 50,
    nullable: true,
  })
  phone?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "phone_verified",
  })
  phoneVerified!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    name: "first_name",
  })
  firstName?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    name: "last_name",
  })
  lastName?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    name: "display_name",
  })
  displayName?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 500,
    nullable: true,
    name: "avatar_url",
  })
  avatarUrl?: string;

  // Password & Security
  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    name: "password_hash",
    select: false, // Don't select by default
  })
  passwordHash!: string;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "password_changed_at",
  })
  passwordChangedAt?: Date;

  @Column({
    type: "boolean",
    default: false,
    name: "force_password_change",
  })
  forcePasswordChange!: boolean;

  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    default: [],
    name: "password_history",
    comment: "Last 5 password hashes to prevent reuse",
  })
  passwordHistory!: string[];

  // Account Status
  @Column({
    type: "enum",
    enum: ["active", "inactive", "suspended", "deleted"],
    default: "active",
  })
  status!: "active" | "inactive" | "suspended" | "deleted";

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "last_login_at",
  })
  lastLoginAt?: Date;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 45,
    nullable: true,
    name: "last_login_ip",
  })
  lastLoginIp?: string;

  // Account Lockout
  @Column({
    type: "int",
    default: 0,
    name: "failed_login_attempts",
  })
  failedLoginAttempts!: number;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "locked_until",
  })
  lockedUntil?: Date;

  // Verification Tokens
  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    name: "email_verification_token",
    select: false,
  })
  emailVerificationToken?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "email_verification_expires_at",
  })
  emailVerificationExpiresAt?: Date;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 255,
    nullable: true,
    name: "password_reset_token",
    select: false,
  })
  passwordResetToken?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "timestamp with time zone",
    nullable: true,
    name: "password_reset_expires_at",
  })
  passwordResetExpiresAt?: Date;

  // Metadata
  @Column({
    type: "jsonb",
    default: {},
    comment: "Additional user metadata",
  })
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 10,
    nullable: true,
    comment: "User timezone (e.g., America/New_York)",
  })
  timezone?: string;

  @Column({
    type: "varchar",
    length: 10,
    default: "en",
    comment: "User preferred language",
  })
  language!: string;

  // Relations
  @OneToOne(() => MfaSettings, (mfa) => mfa.user, {
    cascade: true,
  })
  mfaSettings?: MfaSettings;

  @OneToMany(() => Session, (session) => session.user)
  sessions!: Session[];
}

        "refresh_token_hash" character varying(255),
        "revoked_at" TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
        "revoked_reason" character varying(255),
        CONSTRAINT "PK_sessions" PRIMARY KEY ("id"),
        CONSTRAINT "UQ_sessions_token" UNIQUE ("token_hash"),

          REFERENCES "users"("id") ON DELETE CASCADE
      )

SIZE: 6.32 KB | LINES: 226

import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes, scrypt } from "crypto";
import { promisify } from "util";

const scryptAsync = promisify(scrypt);

/**
 * AES-256-GCM Encryption Service
 *
 * Provides field-level encryption for sensitive data.
 * Uses AES-256-GCM which provides both confidentiality and authenticity.
 */

export interface EncryptionResult {
  encrypted: string; // Base64 encoded
  iv: string; // Base64 encoded initialization vector
  authTag: string; // Base64 encoded authentication tag
}

export class EncryptionService {
  private static readonly ALGORITHM = "aes-256-gcm";
  private static readonly IV_LENGTH = 16; // 128 bits
  private static readonly SALT_LENGTH = 32; // 256 bits
  private static readonly KEY_LENGTH = 32; // 256 bits
  private static readonly AUTH_TAG_LENGTH = 16; // 128 bits

  /**
   * Derive encryption key from master key
   * @param masterKey - Master encryption key
   * @param salt - Salt for key derivation
   * @returns Derived key
   */
  private static async deriveKey(
    masterKey: string,
    salt: Buffer,
  ): Promise<Buffer> {
    return (await scryptAsync(masterKey, salt, this.KEY_LENGTH)) as Buffer;
  }

  /**
   * Encrypt data using AES-256-GCM
   * @param plaintext - Data to encrypt
   * @param masterKey - Master encryption key
   * @param additionalData - Optional authenticated additional data (AAD)
   * @returns Encryption result with IV and auth tag
   */
  static async encrypt(
    plaintext: string,
    masterKey: string,
    additionalData?: string,
  ): Promise<EncryptionResult> {
    // Generate random IV and salt
    const iv = randomBytes(this.IV_LENGTH);
    const salt = randomBytes(this.SALT_LENGTH);

    // Derive encryption key
    const key = await this.deriveKey(masterKey, salt);

    // Create cipher
    const cipher = createCipheriv(this.ALGORITHM, key, iv);

    // Add authenticated additional data if provided
    if (additionalData) {
      cipher.setAAD(Buffer.from(additionalData, "utf8"));
    }

    // Encrypt
    let encrypted = cipher.update(plaintext, "utf8", "base64");
    encrypted += cipher.final("base64");

    // Get authentication tag
    const authTag = cipher.getAuthTag();

    // Combine salt + iv + encrypted data for storage
    const combined = Buffer.concat([
      salt,
      iv,
      Buffer.from(encrypted, "base64"),
    ]);

    return {
      encrypted: combined.toString("base64"),
      iv: iv.toString("base64"),
      authTag: authTag.toString("base64"),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Decrypt data using AES-256-GCM
   * @param encryptedData - Encrypted data object
   * @param masterKey - Master encryption key
   * @param additionalData - Optional authenticated additional data (AAD)
   * @returns Decrypted plaintext
   */
  static async decrypt(
    encryptedData: EncryptionResult | string,
    masterKey: string,
    additionalData?: string,
  ): Promise<string> {
    let encrypted: string;
    let authTag: Buffer;
    let combined: Buffer;

    if (typeof encryptedData === "string") {
      // Legacy format: just the encrypted string
      combined = Buffer.from(encryptedData, "base64");
    } else {
      // New format: object with encrypted, iv, authTag
      combined = Buffer.from(encryptedData.encrypted, "base64");
      authTag = Buffer.from(encryptedData.authTag, "base64");
    }

    // Extract salt, iv, and encrypted data
    const salt = combined.subarray(0, this.SALT_LENGTH);
    const iv = combined.subarray(
      this.SALT_LENGTH,
      this.SALT_LENGTH + this.IV_LENGTH,
    );
    const encryptedBuffer = combined.subarray(
      this.SALT_LENGTH + this.IV_LENGTH,
    );

    // Derive decryption key
    const key = await this.deriveKey(masterKey, salt);

    // Create decipher
    const decipher = createDecipheriv(this.ALGORITHM, key, iv);

    // Set authentication tag
    if (typeof encryptedData !== "string" && authTag!) {
      decipher.setAuthTag(authTag);
    }

    // Add authenticated additional data if provided
    if (additionalData) {
      decipher.setAAD(Buffer.from(additionalData, "utf8"));
    }

    // Decrypt
    let decrypted = decipher.update(encryptedBuffer);
    decrypted = Buffer.concat([decrypted, decipher.final()]);

    return decrypted.toString("utf8");
  }

  /**
   * Encrypt a JSON object
   * @param obj - Object to encrypt
   * @param masterKey - Master encryption key
   * @returns Encrypted result
   */
  static async encryptObject<T>(
    obj: T,
    masterKey: string,
  ): Promise<EncryptionResult> {
    const plaintext = JSON.stringify(obj);
    return this.encrypt(plaintext, masterKey);
  }

  /**
   * Decrypt to JSON object
   * @param encryptedData - Encrypted data
   * @param masterKey - Master encryption key
   * @returns Decrypted object
   */
  static async decryptObject<T>(
    encryptedData: EncryptionResult | string,
    masterKey: string,
  ): Promise<T> {
    const plaintext = await this.decrypt(encryptedData, masterKey);
    return JSON.parse(plaintext);
  }

  /**
   * Generate a secure random key
   * @param length - Key length in bytes (default: 32 for AES-256)
   * @returns Base64 encoded random key
   */
  static generateKey(length: number = this.KEY_LENGTH): string {
    return randomBytes(length).toString("base64");
  }

  /**
   * Hash data using SHA-256
   * @param data - Data to hash
   * @returns Hex encoded hash
   */
  static hash(data: string): string {
    const crypto = require("crypto");
    return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(data).digest("hex");
  }

  /**
   * Generate HMAC signature
   * @param data - Data to sign
   * @param secret - Secret key
   * @returns Hex encoded HMAC
   */
  static hmac(data: string, secret: string): string {
    const crypto = require("crypto");
    return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(data).digest("hex");
  }

  /**
   * Verify HMAC signature
   * @param data - Original data
   * @param signature - HMAC signature to verify
   * @param secret - Secret key
   * @returns True if signature is valid
   */
  static verifyHmac(data: string, signature: string, secret: string): boolean {
    const expected = this.hmac(data, secret);

    // Use timing-safe comparison to prevent timing attacks
    if (expected.length !== signature.length) {
      return false;
    }

    let result = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < expected.length; i++) {
      result |= expected.charCodeAt(i) ^ signature.charCodeAt(i);
    }

    return result === 0;
  }
}

import * as argon2 from "argon2";

/**
 * Password hashing service using Argon2id (OWASP recommended)
 *
 * Argon2id is the winner of the Password Hashing Competition and provides
 * better security than bcrypt against GPU attacks and side-channel attacks.
 */

export interface PasswordHashOptions {
  memoryCost?: number; // Memory cost in KiB (default: 65536 = 64 MB)
  timeCost?: number; // Time cost (iterations, default: 3)
  parallelism?: number; // Parallelism factor (default: 4)
}

export class PasswordService {
  private static readonly DEFAULT_OPTIONS: PasswordHashOptions = {
    memoryCost: 65536, // 64 MB
    timeCost: 3, // 3 iterations
    parallelism: 4, // 4 threads
  };

  /**
   * Hash a password using Argon2id
   * @param password - Plain text password
   * @param options - Hashing options (optional)
   * @returns Hashed password string
   */
  static async hash(
    password: string,
    options: PasswordHashOptions = {},
  ): Promise<string> {
    const opts = { ...this.DEFAULT_OPTIONS, ...options };

    return argon2.hash(password, {
      type: argon2.argon2id, // Use Argon2id variant
      memoryCost: opts.memoryCost,
      timeCost: opts.timeCost,
      parallelism: opts.parallelism,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Verify a password against a hash
   * @param hash - Stored password hash
   * @param password - Plain text password to verify
   * @returns True if password matches, false otherwise
   */
  static async verify(hash: string, password: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      return await argon2.verify(hash, password);
    } catch (error) {
      // Invalid hash format
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Check if a hash needs rehashing (parameters changed)
   * @param hash - Stored password hash
   * @param options - Current hashing options
   * @returns True if hash needs to be updated
   */
  static async needsRehash(
    hash: string,
    options: PasswordHashOptions = {},
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const opts = { ...this.DEFAULT_OPTIONS, ...options };

    return argon2.needsRehash(hash, {
      // type: argon2.argon2id,
      memoryCost: opts.memoryCost,
      timeCost: opts.timeCost,
      parallelism: opts.parallelism,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Validate password strength
   * @param password - Password to validate
   * @returns Object with validation results
   */
  static validateStrength(password: string): {
    isValid: boolean;
    score: number;
    errors: string[];
  } {
    const errors: string[] = [];
    let score = 0;

    // Minimum length check
    if (password.length < 8) {
      errors.push("Password must be at least 8 characters long");
    } else if (password.length >= 8 && password.length < 12) {
      score += 1;
    } else if (password.length >= 12) {
      score += 2;
    }

    // Uppercase check
    if (!/[A-Z]/.test(password)) {
      errors.push("Password must contain at least one uppercase letter");
    } else {
      score += 1;
    }

    // Lowercase check
    if (!/[a-z]/.test(password)) {
      errors.push("Password must contain at least one lowercase letter");
    } else {
      score += 1;
    }

    // Number check
    if (!/\d/.test(password)) {
      errors.push("Password must contain at least one number");
    } else {
      score += 1;
    }

    // Special character check
    if (!/[!@#$%^&*()_+\-=\[\]{};':"\\|,.<>\/?]/.test(password)) {
      errors.push("Password must contain at least one special character");
    } else {
      score += 1;
    }

    // Common patterns check
    const commonPatterns = [
      "password",
      "12345",
      "qwerty",
      "admin",
      "letmein",
      "welcome",
    ];
    if (
      commonPatterns.some((pattern) => password.toLowerCase().includes(pattern))
    ) {
      errors.push("Password contains common patterns");
      score -= 2;
    }

    // Sequential characters check
    if (/(.)\1{2,}/.test(password)) {
      errors.push("Password contains repeated characters");
      score -= 1;
    }

    score = Math.max(0, score); // Ensure score doesn't go below 0

    return {
      isValid: errors.length === 0 && score >= 4,
      score: Math.min(5, score), // Cap at 5
      errors,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Generate a random password
   * @param length - Password length (default: 16)
   * @returns Random secure password
   */
  static generate(length: number = 16): string {
    const uppercase = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";
    const lowercase = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
    const numbers = "0123456789";
    const special = "!@#$%^&*()_+-=[]{}|;:,.<>?";
    const all = uppercase + lowercase + numbers + special;

    let password = "";

    // Ensure at least one of each character type
    password += uppercase[Math.floor(Math.random() * uppercase.length)];
    password += lowercase[Math.floor(Math.random() * lowercase.length)];
    password += numbers[Math.floor(Math.random() * numbers.length)];
    password += special[Math.floor(Math.random() * special.length)];

    // Fill the rest randomly
    for (let i = password.length; i < length; i++) {
      password += all[Math.floor(Math.random() * all.length)];
    }

    // Shuffle the password
    return password
      .split("")
      .sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5)
      .join("");
  }
}

import { Redis } from "ioredis";

/**
 * Sliding Window Rate Limiter
 *
 * Implements a distributed rate limiter using Redis sorted sets.
 * More accurate than fixed window and prevents burst attacks.
 */

export interface RateLimitConfig {
  maxRequests: number; // Maximum requests allowed
  windowMs: number; // Time window in milliseconds
  keyPrefix?: string; // Redis key prefix
}

export interface RateLimitResult {
  allowed: boolean;
  remaining: number;
  resetAt: Date;
  retryAfter?: number; // Seconds until next attempt
}

export class SlidingWindowRateLimiter {
  private redis: Redis;

  constructor(redis: Redis) {
    this.redis = redis;
  }

  /**
   * Check if request is allowed under rate limit
   * @param identifier - Unique identifier (IP, user ID, etc.)
   * @param config - Rate limit configuration
   * @returns Rate limit result
   */
  async isAllowed(
    identifier: string,
    config: RateLimitConfig,
  ): Promise<RateLimitResult> {
    const key = `${config.keyPrefix || "rate_limit"}:${identifier}`;
    const now = Date.now();
    const windowStart = now - config.windowMs;

    // Use Redis pipeline for atomic operations
    const pipeline = this.redis.pipeline();

    // Remove old entries outside the window
    pipeline.zremrangebyscore(key, 0, windowStart);

    // Count requests in current window
    pipeline.zcard(key);

    // Add current request
    pipeline.zadd(key, now, `${now}-${Math.random()}`);

    // Set expiry on the key
    pipeline.expire(key, Math.ceil(config.windowMs / 1000));

    const results = await pipeline.exec();

    if (!results) {
      throw new Error("Redis pipeline execution failed");
    }

    // Get count from the second command (zcard)
    const count = ((results[1] as any)[1] as number) || 0;

    const allowed = count < config.maxRequests;
    const remaining = Math.max(0, config.maxRequests - count - 1);

    let retryAfter: number | undefined;
    if (!allowed) {
      // Get the oldest request timestamp
      const oldest = await this.redis.zrange(key, 0, 0, "WITHSCORES");
      if (oldest.length >= 2) {
        const oldestTimestamp = parseInt(oldest[1] as string);
        const resetTime = oldestTimestamp + config.windowMs;
        retryAfter = Math.ceil((resetTime - now) / 1000);
      }
    }

    return {
      allowed,
      remaining,
      resetAt: new Date(now + config.windowMs),
      retryAfter,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Reset rate limit for an identifier
   * @param identifier - Unique identifier
   * @param keyPrefix - Redis key prefix
   */
  async reset(
    identifier: string,
    keyPrefix: string = "rate_limit",
  ): Promise<void> {
    const key = `${keyPrefix}:${identifier}`;
    await this.redis.del(key);
  }

  /**
   * Get current usage for an identifier
   * @param identifier - Unique identifier
   * @param config - Rate limit configuration
   * @returns Current request count
   */
  async getUsage(identifier: string, config: RateLimitConfig): Promise<number> {
    const key = `${config.keyPrefix || "rate_limit"}:${identifier}`;
    const now = Date.now();
    const windowStart = now - config.windowMs;

    await this.redis.zremrangebyscore(key, 0, windowStart);
    return await this.redis.zcard(key);
  }
}

/**
 * Token Bucket Rate Limiter
 *
 * Allows bursts of traffic while maintaining average rate.
 * Useful for API rate limiting with some flexibility.
 */
export class TokenBucketRateLimiter {
  private redis: Redis;

  constructor(redis: Redis) {
    this.redis = redis;
  }

  /**
   * Check if request is allowed and consume token
   * @param identifier - Unique identifier
   * @param bucketSize - Maximum tokens in bucket
   * @param refillRate - Tokens added per second
   * @param cost - Tokens consumed per request (default: 1)
   * @returns Rate limit result
   */
  async consume(
    identifier: string,
    bucketSize: number,
    refillRate: number,
    cost: number = 1,
  ): Promise<RateLimitResult> {
    const key = `token_bucket:${identifier}`;
    const now = Date.now();

    const data = await this.redis.get(key);
    let tokens = bucketSize;
    let lastRefill = now;

    if (data) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
      tokens = parsed.tokens;
      lastRefill = parsed.lastRefill;

      // Calculate tokens to add based on time elapsed
      const timePassed = (now - lastRefill) / 1000; // seconds
      const tokensToAdd = timePassed * refillRate;
      tokens = Math.min(bucketSize, tokens + tokensToAdd);
    }

    const allowed = tokens >= cost;
    let remaining = tokens;

    if (allowed) {
      remaining = tokens - cost;
      await this.redis.setex(
        key,
        Math.ceil(bucketSize / refillRate) + 60, // TTL
        JSON.stringify({
          tokens: remaining,
          lastRefill: now,
        }),
      );
    }

    const resetAt = new Date(
      now + ((bucketSize - remaining) / refillRate) * 1000,
    );
    const retryAfter = allowed
      ? undefined
      : Math.ceil((cost - tokens) / refillRate);

    return {
      allowed,
      remaining: Math.floor(remaining),
      resetAt,
      retryAfter,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Reset token bucket for an identifier
   * @param identifier - Unique identifier
   */
  async reset(identifier: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.redis.del(`token_bucket:${identifier}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Distributed Leaky Bucket Rate Limiter
 *
 * Processes requests at a constant rate, queuing excess requests.
 * Best for smoothing out traffic spikes.
 */
export class LeakyBucketRateLimiter {
  private redis: Redis;

  constructor(redis: Redis) {
    this.redis = redis;
  }

  /**
   * Check if request can be processed
   * @param identifier - Unique identifier
   * @param capacity - Maximum bucket capacity
   * @param leakRate - Requests processed per second
   * @returns Rate limit result
   */
  async isAllowed(
    identifier: string,
    capacity: number,
    leakRate: number,
  ): Promise<RateLimitResult> {
    const key = `leaky_bucket:${identifier}`;
    const now = Date.now();

    const data = await this.redis.get(key);
    let level = 0;
    let lastLeak = now;

    if (data) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
      level = parsed.level;
      lastLeak = parsed.lastLeak;

      // Calculate how much leaked since last check
      const timePassed = (now - lastLeak) / 1000; // seconds
      const leaked = timePassed * leakRate;
      level = Math.max(0, level - leaked);
    }

    const allowed = level < capacity;

    if (allowed) {
      level += 1;
      await this.redis.setex(
        key,
        Math.ceil(capacity / leakRate) + 60, // TTL
        JSON.stringify({
          level,
          lastLeak: now,
        }),
      );
    }

    const remaining = Math.floor(capacity - level);
    const resetAt = new Date(now + (level / leakRate) * 1000);
    const retryAfter = allowed
      ? undefined
      : Math.ceil((level - capacity + 1) / leakRate);

    return {
      allowed,
      remaining,
      resetAt,
      retryAfter,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Reset leaky bucket for an identifier
   * @param identifier - Unique identifier
   */
  async reset(identifier: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.redis.del(`leaky_bucket:${identifier}`);
  }
}
